krypton, 5 years ago.

 

Helena hated visiting the capital, it was always the same crowd of people, the same smell and the same people trying their best to get close to her, looking for favors and if there was one thing Helena hated was stupidity and flattery.

"Helena!" She heard Astra calling and ran towards her, hugging her lightly. The woman was the Queen’s twin sister but to Helena she was just the woman from whom she had first learned about strategies and fights.

"Astra" She sighed, relieved to find a familiar face "How are things here?"

"Well..." The general looked around taking her to a far corner of the palace's entrance gardens "Tensions are rising, you know that not everyone approves of your majesty Zor-el"

Helena sighed and nodded, Krypton was always led by the elderly families but after the rebellion only their families, Kieran and Zor-el, remained in power and even today Helena doesn’t know how it was decided between them but it was agreed that Zor-el would sit on the throne and that Kierans would be his heirs and advisers. The problem with this decision was that the Zor-el family was known to be peaceful and calm and Krypton was at war.

"The people cry out for you, Helena, you and your mother" Astra sighed. Everyone knew that her family was actually leading the war, Helena had just returned from Daxam. She shuddered just to remember "This is dividing our population and it is dividing our allies"

"My mother would never take the throne from Zor-el" Helena shook her head walking beside the general. For her birth right, Helena had the right to walk in front of her but she never did it out of respect for the woman she considered family. "They are friends, they treat each other like family..."

"Helena, don't get me wrong..." Astra pulled her by the arm "I am not trying to creating a coup against my own sister’s husband... I’m only saying that Kieran and Zor-el need to reach an agreement or our allies will abandon us"

"Does your majesty still think you can convince Rhea?" Helena frowned, Astra was not wrong.

"Yes!" She said in exasperation "My brother-in-law thinks that woman can change her mind, that we can find a peaceful solution! Tell me you don't believe that, Helena"

"Of course I don't believe it" Helena snorted and tugged at the sleeve of her dress to show a big cut on her arm "I just left Daxam, Rhea killed fifteen of my soldiers! Fifteen!" Helena was annoyed "That's why I came back, I came to ask permission to attack first this time"

"He won't let you..." Astra shook her head "You need...."

"Helena!!!!" The two turned when they saw Kara coming towards them, surrounded by security guards! Helena looked in Astra’s direction and she understood, the subject has been closed for now.

"Kara" She smiled towards her. The two had known each other since childhood, with the proximity of their families it was impossible not to and although she sometimes found the blonde a little irritating, Helena liked her company. Of course, now the two hardly saw each other, since Helena was always on other planets trying to avoid war while Kara was... She didn't know what she’s doing. "How are you?"

"I'm great" The blonde smiled "I joined the scientific council, did Aunt Astra tell you?"

"I didn't get a chance," Astra said beside her.

"Ah yes!" Kara looked at her confused "Anyway, I'm loving it"

"How nice" Helena nodded being polite.

"Are you going to stay long? Can we catch up?"

"Actually, I need to speak to your majesty, your father" Helena said, thinking of the blood dripping from her face when they left Daxam. "It’s very important"

"Of course!"

Helena, Kara and Astra then proceeded to the palace. They were directed to the red wing, one that Helena was beginning to become very familiar with all the times she had been here.

"How good it is to see you" His majesty Zor-el said when he saw them "Kara, my love, why don't you go find your mother and tell her that we will have company for dinner?"

"Isn't she better off staying?" Helena asked intrigued, one day Kara would sit on the Krypton throne, wasn't it better for her to know what was going on with her planet?

"Maybe later" He said with a tone of finality and Helena didn't argue, well, there was that.

Kara had just turned to go when one of the doors opened and some armed men entered the hall and started shooting with weapons that Helena had never seen in all her training.

Quickly the brunette pulled out her own weapons in reflex, trying her best to protect herself and those who were beside her. Helena cursed herself for coming dressed in a not so practical way, for letting her guard down...

"Helena" Astra called her, she was already pulling the King behind her and Helena got the message. Catching Kara around the waist, she headed out into the hall, she knew men were chasing them.

"What is happening?" The blonde asked but Helena didn't answer, she was too busy trying to dodge the weapons around her and trying to hit them back. She just hoped that Astra had the same idea.

Helena knew that the training center was at the end of that corridor on the left, she had trained there for many years and could follow this path with her eyes closed.

"Helena!! We have to help my father" Kara spoke and was ignored. The men were very close now and Helena had to turn to face them.

She counted three, it was possible, she’d fought in the worst conditions... She needed to get rid of the weapons in their hands, aiming as much as possible at the hand of the nearest man. She shot and yes, she got it right. His weapon fell beside her and Helena moved towards him, she couldn't hesitate now.

He struggled a little but the brunette could see that whoever it was was not highly trained, he tried to hit her in the stomach but she dodged and it gave her the opportunity to trap him from behind, with her right arm around his neck she turned him around at the exact moment when the other shot, making the man her human shield.

Without thinking, Helena advanced on the other two men using the body of the first as a shield. She could feel the projectile piercing his abdomen and felt him go limp, completely unconscious. Taking advantage of the opportunity, she threw his body in one of the attackers’ way and jumped at the other at the same time, her right leg straight on his neck making him fall on his knees.

Helena broke his neck without thinking twice.

Now with only one of the attackers alive, she knew she could play... Smiling, she walked towards him as he tried desperately to search for his weapon. Helena kicked the object away and put a knee on his neck.

"Who do you work for?" She asked in a whisper.

Silence.

"Last chance to get out alive" She said calmly "Who do you work for?"

He remained silent.

"Very well" Helena used her anger to break his neck too and observed his body lifeless on the floor.

"Rao!" She heard Kara exclaim and she turned to see several generals standing at the training center, probably Kara kept running to call them while she dealt with the men.

"General Kieran" One of them called her "Where's your majesty?"

Helena's eyes widened, remembering that Astra and Zor-el were still alone, why? She didn't understand... Astra had clearly given her a signal to protect Kara while she would protect Zor-el.

Helena didn’t answer them, just got up and ran in the opposite direction, the soldiers and some generals followed.

It didn't take long for her to hear the blonde scream beside her. Arriving in the red room, they could see that Zor-el was dead and the killers had already fled.

Helena sighed and looked around for Astra, founding only her body unconscious, she had been hit and was bleeding.

"We need to take her to the medical wing" Helena said quickly without caring that a King had just died.

"Rao..." One of the generals who accompanied her said "Kara Zor-el will ascend"

Helena blinked while carrying Astra, yes, for Rao... Kara? Would KARA be Queen of the Empire?

"Don't just stand there" Helena said irritably, they were all paralyzed "Start searching the palace, the assassins shouldn't be far away... GO" Everyone ran out of the halls, except for the generals who were still looking at the scene in shock. Yes, Kara would ascend but it was Helena they’re taking orders from as Kara was hugging her father's body, unable to even give orders and everyone was looking at Helena for instructions.

"Help me take her" She said when they finished fixing Astra so she could be carried. Helena didn’t know the severity of her wounds and was not willing to wait until the medical team got here, she would carry Astra herself if necessary.

"I’ll take her" One of the strongest generals said "I can carry her for longer with less turbulence"

Helena nodded and turned back to where Kara was, devastated.

Kara just lost her father and had won in return a empire on the brick of war to rule in return.

The brunette can still remember the taste of the wine she took at her coronation, the taste of the promises she made and obliged, the taste of her own blood as she endured one of the worst pains of her life and the taste of the blood of her enemies splashing from her own sword.

Yes, Alex Danvers is right, she has a behavior problem but only Helena knows that she has to be like this to survive, she has to attack first always or dozens of lives would be on her.

She can’t let anything go and she can’t trust, that's what she was trained for, to not to be wrong and today she failed... She’s been failing since she appeared in this strange universe where everything was upside down, where her body was not her own.

Nothing made sense, her instincts don’t help her, her mind doesn’t work the same way and yet Helena still wakes up breathless in the midst of nightmares about her enemies.

Astra would call her weak, tell her to control her thoughts but she knows now that the weight of everything that’s happening had finally settled and now she has to deal with the consequences, and after all, that’s not her specialty.

Put her on a battlefield and Helena will come out victorious every time but a battle against her own feelings? She wasn't so sure anymore.

 

—————————

"You may come in" She hears the permission and drags the big doors until she can get through. "Your Majesty..." The woman in front of her stood up in respect.

Lena shakes her head and motions for her to sit.

"Layla" She says the old woman's name for the first time "Call me Lena"

"Lena?" The woman is clearly confuse “Your Highness never liked nicknames"

"I insist" Lena says accustomed to people being surprised by this but she refuses to be called Majesty, Highness or Helena.

"Alright" The older one looked at her suspiciously "How mayI help you?"

"Layla... We both know we have a past together" Lena throws in the bait “I think maybe we could talk a little bit about it..."

"After all these years?" She’s genuinely surprise "What has changed?"

"I changed" Lena admits "You must have heard about the attacks?"

"Several times" She nods "But I heard that, I mean, you got rid of them all with mastery"

"You trained me" Lena watches her and sees the woman nod again.

"I wasn’t the only one, but I like to consider myself a good influence"

"Unlike other people?" Lena raises an eyebrow, feeling a certain animosity in her speech.

"You already know what I think"

"Refresh my memory" Lena ordered more than she asked and saw the older woman sigh before starting to speak.

"You know I think she caused the intrigue between the two of us" Layla admits "I'm sure she made up that story with my husband, he would never do that, Helena, you wouldn't even let him explain"

Lena watches her in silence.

"Years of build trust and for what? To be discarded in the first word of that snake" Now she had caught a stir and Lena could see that the woman was pouring out everything that she had wanted to say all these years and Helena did not allow it. "I was left out without a word of affection from you, without your consideration... We were banished from our city, even worse, from your life"

"Layla..."

"All of this so that Astra could have you under her control, she knew you were still listening to me... That's why she invented that lie"

Lena knows instinctively that she can’t ask what lie, she would not be able to get the whole story out of the old woman without surrendering or demonstrating that something had happened but she was also not sure that she can trust her word without proof...

She doesn’t know if she can trust Astra either, but at least the general is not a stranger and so far, she had kept her secret.

But at what price?

"Say you forgive us" Lena came back to reality with the request and froze, she doesn’t know if she can forgive her, not without knowing the whole story even though she felt a certain truth in her words. "Undo the exile and let us return to the Capital and everything can be as it was before"

Lena looked at her doubtfully, what would Helena do in this situation?

Deep down she knows; Helena would have simply turned away and be happy with the old woman's suffering but Lena was not Helena.

"I can only promise to think about it”
But she also would not commit without further evidence as to what actually happened.

Lena gets up and turns to leave but not before hearing the older woman's calm and melodic voice.

"I know that you will make the right decision in the end”

She hopes so.

everything’s wrong

"When were you going to tell me you’re in love with Lena?" Alex asked as soon as she pulled her sister from the room where they were.

"What?" Kara's eyes widened "In love? Me? Alex!!!"

"You don't have to pretend" The brunette rolls her eyes "I can't believe I didn’t see it before... That's why you're so worried!"

"Hey!" Kara protests crossing her arms "I'm worried because I care about Lena, not because I'm in love with her”

"Kara, you don't have to lie" Alex sighs "It's me, Alex! You don't have to lie to me"

"I ..." Kara hesitates biting her lips "I... I don’t really know... My feelings were changing and then she found out about Supergirl and then she wasn’t here anymore and I... I don’t know”

Kara shakes her head and leans against the wall, uncrossing her arms and sitting on the floor in the DEO corridor, Alex soon crouched down beside her.

"We’re going to bring her back, you’ll see..." Alex tried to cheer her up "And then you’ll tell her everything and it will be okay..."

"But what if she doesn't want to talk to me? Alex, she knows that I lied to her, she knows everything now"

The agent frowns, Lena's her friend too and she’d lied to her.

"It was the right thing to do, for her protection and yours" Alex says firmly "She will understand sooner or later"

"I don't know what I'm going to do if she doesn’t forgive me..." Kara swallows the cry and Alex feels her heart squeeze by her sister's agony "If she looks at me the way Helena does...” A shiver runs down her spine.

"No Kara! Lena would never do that! Helena’s a pain in the ass, you know that”

"She has her reasons" Kara shrugs her shoulders and Alex rolls her eyes, there was no version of the CEO that the blonde didn't like, not even the most unbearable one.

"She’s rude and inconsequential, I can see why her world is at war, Helena’s highly unstable"

"My planet is at war because the alternative version of your sister is a traitor who comes from a family of cowards who did nothing while their world was broken into heavyweights by their enemies"

The two sisters turns around to see that Helena had left the room and was listening to the end of the conversation. Alex looks at her sister with irritation, what good was it to have a superior hearing ability if she could not even hear a person approaching a few inches back?

"I'm not going to argue with you, Helena" Alex snorts angrily and looks at her younger sister "We will talk later, ok?"

Kara nods and watches as the agent leave their presence as quickly as possible.

"Thank you for defending me but it is not necessary" Kara looks at the brunette when she hears the confession.

"Of course it is. And it’s not like it was a lie... You have your reasons, even though I think they’re not as real as you think”

Helena rolls her eyes.

"Give up while you're still ahead"

"Sure" Kara smirks and stands up, now face to face with the woman who had stolen a kiss a few moments ago... She doesn’t know what to say. "Err ..."

"No need to be embarrassed" Helena says looking back "We were just testing a theory"

"And I, I mean, won't the other Kara care?" Kara would definitely care if her wife kissed someone else, theory or no theory.

"Of course she won’t, you know that things are not like that between the two of us”

"Helena..."

"Please do not insist on this matter, my head is exploding and the last thing I need is your conspiracy and murder theories"

"You already know what I think..." Kara says defeated, she really doesn’t how to get the brunette to listen to her "Tell me at least if you ever talked to your Kara about it? Did you confront her?"

Helena narrows her eyes.

"If I answer, you promise you’ll stop bringing this up?”

"I promise not to mention it anymore" Kara smiles "Not today, anyway”

"Small victories I guess..." Helena sighs "No, Supergirl, of course I didn't talk to her about it. As soon as Kara Zor-el knows that I know everything I an dead"

"I would never do that" Kara swore solemnly "Never!"

"But you are not the same person" Helena smirks “Just like Lena and I"

Upon hearing Lena's name, her anguish comes back twice as hard.

"Do you think she's okay? Lena?" She frowns asking.

"Ah! She looks fine!" Helena snorts sarcastically "Kissing my wife, in my palace... Probably in my bed! Rao!! How will I explain this to my maids I don't know... Can you imagine the rumors running through the corridors of that place ? Maybe even on the streets of the capital?"

"How bad can it be?" Kara laughs and shrugs "You guys are married, at least that's what everyone thinks, what's wrong with that?" Apart from the part that Kara doesn’t really know how to feel about Lena being involved with someone else she’s rational enough to know that the brunette is single and free to do whatever she likes.

Everything that’s happening is in some way her fault and Kara can’t blame Lena for being with someone else.

Even if that other person is an alternative version of herself.

And a married one at that!! Okay, so Kara can judge her a little bit on that part. And she would do that, as soon as Lena comes home.

 

"It's my reputation that is at stake, Supergirl" Helena says in exasperation as Kara rambled "If my enemies find out that I am hanging out with my wife for kisses instead of planning an attack on them, that leaves gaps, leaves opportunities for them to attack us, while we’re distracted”

"Rhea? Do you think she would use that against you?"

"Of course she would use it" Helena rolls her eyes "But Rhea is not my only enemy, within my counsel... Scattered throughout the Capital... Everywhere there are people planning and waiting for at a hint of distraction to put a dagger in my back"

"I only care about Lena in the middle of all this” Kara frowns.

"Well... she hasn't died so far” Helena tries to comfort the woman in the only way she knows how.

"I... I like her" Kara murmurs and the queen raises an eyebrow "You were right the other day... When you said that I care about Lena, that I have feelings for her..."

"And she for you ..."

"What?" Kara blinks quickly "Did she say anything when you guys saw each other? Did she mention me?"

"She didn't even mention your name" Helena says the true as always and sees the blonde adopt the defeated posture, making her feel a little pity and remorse "However," Kara looks into her eyes "If she wasn't interested in any part of you she wouldn't be kissing a Kara from another universe, would she? It's very simple to understand if you stop to think about it”

"But you said yourself that we’re different people" Kara frowns in confusion but the brunette just sighs in her direction.

"Not that different" Helena looks at her from head to toe "Not that much”

 

—————————-

 

"You're upset with me" Lena realizes when she stops beside the blonde in the lounge. The two are waiting for Sam, Reign here actually, in the main hall of the hotel where they were staying and since Lena got here, Kara had avoided looking in her direction.

"Why would be?" Kara asks still without looking at Lena and she sighs losing some patience.

"Don't be passive aggressive, Kara" She says firmly "If I did something that hurt you, tell me"

"Okay" The blonde looks at her from the corner of her eye and with one last look in the direction of Ruby, who was entertained with Ingio from across the room she continues "You told me no yesterday"

"And?" Lena raises an eyebrow at her to continue but Kara says nothing more "Is that it?"

"Don’t you think that’s enough?" Kara murmurs angrily.

"Of course not!" Lena snorts angrily "We can't always have everything we want when we want it"

"Look who's talking..." Kara turned towards her finally "You are the most spoiled person I know, Helena" She purposely calls her Helena and Lena narrows her eyes.

"And you need to learn to hear the word ‘no’!"

"Argh!!" Kara pulls her own hair "I don't understand... Everything was going well, why did you reject me?"

Lena disarms herself when she hears the confusion in her tone, it was never her intention to play with the woman's feelings but in a way that was what she had done... Lena knows she’s keeping secrets, she knows that at some point she’ll have to return to her reality and yet she’d let herself get involved with Kara.

Perhaps because of an absurd need to escape from her new reality or to forget her old one.

Ever since she arrived this Kara has been so interested, so in need of Lena's presence that it was easy to allow herself to sink into her presence and yesterday she had seen that it wasn't fair to anyone, least of all to herself.

"I was trying to do the right thing..." Lena sighs "It's not fair of me to build something based on lies”

"Lies?" Kara still sounded confused but now she is more alert "What are you talking about?"

Lena sighs wearily. Will she really do that? Tell the truth?

"There are things about me that you don't know..." She hesitates "I ..."

"Your Majesties!! They’re coming" They’re interrupted by Ingio's irritating voice announcing the arrival of Reign, accompanied of course by Astra. They can already see them from afar now that the doors were open.

"Lena? What are you talking about?"

Lena shakes her head and walks towards Ruby, the girl’s very nervous and the brunette take this as a sign that she was about to do something crazy. She’d almost told Kara everything right there, in the heat of the moment... What the hell was she thinking?

"Not now" She offered as an explanation "Ruby need us," She calms them both "Nervous?"

"A little" The girl says biting her lips, clearly more nervous than she said she was. In the corner of her vision she sees Kara approaching and broad shoulders standing next to her.

 

Anyone looking from the outside could even think they’re a happy family, the imposing and serious blonde on Lena's right side, because it is the facade that Kara Zor-el shows in public. Lena, who, contrary to Helena's killer look, carries with her an aura of calming wisdom knowing that she’s about to reunite mother and daughter and, of course, Ruby between the two, like a daughter leaving to go to college in another state.

This is the scene that Astra and Reign sees as they pass the large golden doorway of the hotel, Ingio closing the doors behind them.

Lena tries hard to recognize her friend behind the brown eyes in front of her, tries to recognize any kind of flash or feeling but all she sees inside Sam is resentment and soon her calmness begins to be replace by apprehension.

"In Rao's name, Helena, why did you summon me here?" Sam, she means, Reign asks acidly towards her and she feels as everyone in the room goes rigid. Does she know Helena?

"Reign..." Lena tries to buy time to think of a strategy, what would she do now? How to explain this situation?

"Do you know each other?" To her surprise, Astra is also looking at the situation with interest.

"Do we know...?" Reign snorts indignantly "I gave her my daughter, she promised me that she would take care of her but I always thought you threw her in the trash when I saw that you were about to marry royalty" Reign made a small bow playing with the title of the two women.

“Helena was already royalty herself” Astra says but is ignored.

"Lena? Is that how you knew her name?" Kara asks calmly but it is Ruby who has her attention. The girl looks between the two quickly, her head moving in denial.

"You knew?" The teenager gasps at the words and Lena swallows hard "You knew all this time that she didn't want me and you made me believe it could have been a mistake... You made me believe that someone would want me! I HATE YOU!" The girl screams in Lena's direction and rans out of the room.

"I ... I'm going after her" Kara says and quickly leaves in the same direction.

Lena’s in shock standing in the same spot, she doesn’t know what to do... How to get out of this situation? It’s obvious that Reign and Helena had some kind of past and she’d no idea how to start to unfold this spider's nest that is Helena's life.

Astra doesn’t seem to be able to help either, she just stands there looking at the both of them as some kind of strange experiment, curiosity and suspicion in her eyes.

"So you kept the bastard"

"Don't talk about her like that!" Lena got angry. Regardless of what universe she’s in, Ruby’s still her goddaughter and she’ll protect her from everything, even her own mother.

Not that she can call the woman in front of her a mother... In fact, she can’t even recognize Sam in her eyes or in her posture or even in her features. Sam is kind and friendly, good even while she struggled with darkness inside her...

Yes, that was it! In Krypton there was no reason for her soul to fight, there is no Sam... Only Reign and she’d to remember that for Reign, feelings are weaknesses.

"Why did you bring me here? Why do this show, Helena?"

"It’s Your Majesty" Astra interrupts "If you continue to speak to your majesty in a rude way I will be forced to arrest you”

"Astra..." Lena sighs, sometimes the general goes too far "It's okay, leave us alone please"

"Over my dead body, which may be soon" The older woman snorts "Do you have a death wish, Helena?" She stress the name.

"The guards are going to be right there at the end of the hall"

"Lena..."

"It's an order, Astra" It was the first time that Lena had used that tone with the woman and she calculatedly observed what her reaction would be.

Astra just nods and leaves the room quietly.

"Look at you... giving orders and everything" Reign scoffs and looks around "Your life is very good, that's why you don't remember your old friends"

Lena swallows and decides to throw some food to see if she would take the bait, from what she saw from the woman with Ruby she doesn’t have much maternal instinct.

"You said you didn't want to hear from the girl" Her heart speeds up as Reign pauses.

"You're right" Lena exhales “Even so ... I expected at least one letter sporadically"

Lena raises an eyebrow, maybe she and Helena were lovers? She needs a way to find out...

"I'm married now"

"Did you lost the right to write letters after you married?”

Not lovers then...
Lena sighs, this is too risky, she can’t go on with this little game of information.

"Enough jokes, Helena. Why did you bring me here?"

"I thought it was time for Ruby to know the truth, to know her origins, to meet her mother"

"I'm not anyone's mother" Reign snorts "If that's all I better go"

"Reign, wait" Lena put a hand on her forearm to stop her from going and she watched from the corner of her eyes as her bodyguards moved in response. It reminded her that she has to talk to Astra about the changes in her personal security. “Don't you at least want to talk to her?”

"I prefer not to" The woman sighs, now that they’re alone she was being more open and her posture was very reminiscent of someone Lena knows... "It will only make things even more difficult for her and me"

"Reign!" Lena tries to call her but the woman is already heading for the exit.

Lena almost cries out in frustration.
Everything is going so absurdly wrong in this goddamn trip.

Conspiracies, theories and forbidden kisses.

"Lena!!"  She heard Astra calling as she hurried through the hotel's modern corridors, her only concern was finding Ruby and trying, not knowing how, to explain what had happened...

 

Even if she didn't even know it.

 

"Lena! Wait!"

 

“Can't wait" She turned around angrily and saw the woman right behind her "I have to talk to Ruby"

 

“And tell her what? That you lied? That you had no idea who her mother was?"

 

“Astra, don't piss me off" Lena huffed and kept walking.

 

"I'm serious, Lena..." The general took her arm "You can't tell her that, you'll ruin everything"

 

"Everything?"  Lena threw her arms up in despair "What exactly am I trying to protect here with this lie? I should have told the truth as soon as I opened my eyes on this planet..."

 

"You know very well what we have to protect!"  Astra said in a very serious way, not believing that Lena was even doubting at this point "You know what's at stake..."

 

"No! Astra! I don't know!"  Lena kept raising her voice "I just know what you told me"

 

"So you don't trust me, is that it?"  The general squinted her eyes "After all I've done for you? After keeping your secrets?"

 

"No one trusts you, Astra," Lena admitted hotly from her argument with Sa...Reign.  "Why should I?"

 

Astra looked at her with surprise in her gaze and took a step back, physically and mentally away from the brunette and Lena swallowed hard, trying to calm down.  She didn't want to alienate a person who, up until now, had kept her secret but after everything she'd heard in that reality, she didn't know if she could trust Astra blindly the way she had when she first arrived.

 

"Why should you?"  Astra gasped repeating her words "For... I don't need to stay here and justify myself to a stranger!"

 

"Convenient..." Lena huffed "Helena might trust you but I don't! For me you know more than you say about the attempts on her life"

 

Lena was no longer mincing words, her game with Astra had ceased to be subtle and now she spoils by entering into open combat.

 

"Don't you dare doubt me like that!"  The woman said indignantly, her face lost all color "I love Helena! There is no one on this planet who knows her better than I do! I would never do anything that would put her life at risk..." Astra sounded extremely offended "On the contrary of the people you've been… close to"

 

"What are you talking about?"  Lena hesitated at the general's tone of voice, she sounded genuinely surprised.  Could Helena be right?

 

"About my niece of course! I never told you for your own safety but I think it's time you learned the whole truth about Helena's marriage."

 

"I know the whole truth..."

 

"You don't know anything"

 

"You told me the marriage was arranged because of the war... That Helena didn't want to get married"

 

Astra sighed and looked around, the two were still standing in the hallway and this wasn't a safe enough place to have this conversation.

 

"Not here," She pulled the brunette back into the hall "Go inside"

 

Lena hesitated at the door but decided to follow the general into the hall, after all, Helena trusted her blindly and therefore she deserved at least the benefit of the doubt.

 

Astra quickly closed the doors as she entered and Lena wondered if that had been a good idea... The general sat up and she relaxed.

 

"Where to start?"

 

"The truth Astra" Lena said also sitting down "I want the truth"

 

"Very well" She sighed "Did I tell you about the attack that took King Zor-el's life and put Kara in power?"

 

"No" Lena frowned, she had read about it in the Capital library but she didn't know the details.

 

"Well then, it all started with a rather clumsy attempt to sign a peace agreement with Daxam. Helena's father was on one of the missions to recruit allies on neighboring planets, more specifically on Belfok, a planet close to Daxam."

 

"I read about it" Lena frowned trying to remember the book "Rhea attacked the ship they were in, correct?"

 

"Yes" Astra sighed running a hand through her hair "But what isn't in the books is that it was Kara Zor-el who gave Rhea the ship's location"

 

"I don't believe it" Lena blinked in shock.

 

"Helena didn't believe it either! Rao, I didn't believe it!"  Astra said in exasperation getting up and pacing "You think it doesn't hurt to know that my own niece was involved in this? As good as her intentions had been... Helena can never forgive her and I can't either”

 

"Maybe she had good intentions? Did she try to make a deal?"  Lena was trying to find any explanation.

 

"Yes, she was naive... Rhea said she wanted to find the mission, that she wanted to make peace"

 

"So she was misled?"  Lena felt relieved "It Wasn't her intention to kill anyone, right?"

 

"No... Not yet" Astra hesitated "After that, we found out that she was behind the attack on then King Zor-el, she gave access to the assassins to enter the red wing of the palace. When the Daxionian confirmed I could barely believe it, I needed proof... I found documents signed in the old-fashioned way by her, documents that made a new deal with Rhea, for peace..."

 

"And these documents could not be forged?"  Lena arched an eyebrow, what else could happen...

 

"The old-fashioned way, Lena... It means they were signed by blood in front of the Matrix, there's no way to fake something like that."

 

Lena frowned and looked away from the general, taking in the ceiling decorated like the rest of the hotel in a modern vintage fashion, it was as if Lena lived in the Middle Ages with the technology of the future.  She was getting distracted…distracting herself from thinking about the information Astra was putting in her hands, what she was saying felt right in paper but not in her heart.

 

Everything in Lena screamed that Kara was somehow innocent in this whole mess.  At least the Kara she knows could never be involved in this way...

 

On the other hand, from the little she knew of Queen Kara, she knew that for the sake of the greater good, she might at some point have sat at this table to talk and Lena didn't know how to feel about that.

 

She profusely understood Helena's irritation with the blonde and could admit that the situation was at the very least complicated, yet at the same time she could only judge from her own experience and the experiences she had with Kara from this universe led her to believe that something was wrong in this story.

 

"I'm going to want to see these documents, I want to see all the evidence that what you say is true"

 

"Of course" Astra readily agreed. "I'm not asking you to trust me blindly Lena, just be careful what you say to my niece. Like I said, she has a good heart but sometimes she doesn't know that what’s she’s doing is wrong"

 

"I can only promise not to tell anything until I see this evidence" Lena had kept it a secret until now, she could wait a little longer and make sure she could trust the Queen.  "Then, depending on how I judge the situation, I'll tell her the truth, Astra, I'm tired of lying"

 

"Alright" The general agreed "It's the best I can ask for"

 

Lena nodded and without thinking too much she headed out into the hallway again, this time calmer and with no one following her.  Her head spun thinking about the things that Astra's said to her as well as the behavior of Reign. She got into the panoramic elevator wondering how she was going to explain all of this to Ruby and Kara without telling the truth but how could she tell the truth with so many doubts about the blonde's character being raised?

 

If she could just talk to Helena right now...

 

"Wow" Lena jumped when she heard her own voice very slightly beside her and looked in the direction of a rather ghostly figure.

 

Beside her was the image of Helena as if she were being projected from a very distant place.

 

"Wait, you're not sleeping..." Helena squinted her eyes towards her "Are you getting in touch while awake? How did you do that? It's an extremely advanced technique."

 

"I don't know" Lena sighed trying to focus on the brunette's image but the two didn't seem to be seeing each other very well "I just thought about you and that's it"

 

"Very good Lena" Helena let out a rare compliment "What happened?"

 

"Astra"

 

"I can't believe you're using a highly sophisticated technique so we can fight over my general" Helena rolled her eyes.

 

"She told me some things about Kara, her Kara..."

 

"Did she tell you about my father?"

Lena nodded and blinked when she heard the noise of the elevator reaching its destination and quickly pressed the button to keep the doors closed, she needed more time.

 

"Do you think it was her? Is that why you hate her?"

 

"The evidence is irrefutable Lena" Helena shook her head "I can't forgive her, I can't forget... And she still makes me marry her after all this?"

 

"I don't believe this story" Lena said gently "Even if she was really involved, from the way Astra spoke she didn't quite know what consequences it would have"

 

"Since when do I care about her intentions? What matters is what she did"

 

Helena's image began to shake and Lena felt such weakness in her legs that she had to support herself on the side of the elevator.  Slowly sliding down to the ground.

 

"You're losing your mind" Helena clucked her tongue in disapproval "Be careful with your questions and don't confront Kara if you have any love for your life and in Rao's name, stop kissing my wife!!"

 

Lena didn't know whether to laugh or cry when she heard the last sentence and Helena disappeared.  This was her life now...

 

Conspiracies, theories and forbidden kisses.

 

One way or another she was already made up, she was going to investigate this story told by Astra and discover the truth once and for all.

 

They all deserved to know.

confrontation

"How could you be so cruel, Helena?" She heard as she returned to the suite where they were staying "I thought you changed, I believed you! It was all a lie, a joke, wasn't it?"

Lena sighed at the blonde's accusations, she couldn't defend herself without telling the whole truth and after the conversation she had with Astra she didn't know if it was wise to do that at this point.

Anyway, her priority was another...

"Where's Ruby?" She started to search the room, going to the doors that led to the side.

"No!" Kara grabbed her arm and pulled her back into the middle of the room, leaving her surprised "You're not going to talk to her right now! She's devastated, Helena, doesn't want to see you."

Lena felt her heart clench.
"But I need to talk to her"

“No! What you need to do is tell me the truth" Kara said seriously, her tone recalling Lena's first interactions with blondes in this universe. "Because you made us come here after Ruby's birth mother, if she can even be called a mother, for what? For her to be rejected"

"I didn't know she would reject Ruby" Lena tried to defend herself but Kara shook her head.

“You knew her..." She continued "She said she gave you Ruby as a baby Helena, you left the girl in that orphanage"

“I…" Lena hesitated, she didn't have the answers the blonde wanted, only Helena could tell the truth as only she knew the truth. Lena bit her lip, everything around her seemed to close in on her. Lena lost her sense of space a little, the only thing making her lucid was Kara's grip on her arm. At another time Lena would complain that she would be hurting her but now it was her only point of salvation.

"So? Helena! You owe me an explanation!!"

Lena blinked and felt a shiver run down her spine and heard a loud hum.

“No, dear wife," The two turned to the voice coming from the back of the suite, standing with a pose that only a true queen could have, was Helena. "I think you are the one that owns me explanations"

Kara released Lena's arm in shock, her wide eyes darting between the two women in front of her.

Helena moved closer and Lena could see that her skin was glowing, like a few moments ago in the elevador, just this once, much stronger. Lena could taste the connection between them, it felt unbreakable...

"Helena" Lena sighed confused.

“I knew your lack of control would lead us to this" The woman looked in her direction ignoring the shocked blonde beside her. Kara kept silent trying to understand what was going on in front of her "You forced a connection with me while touching her..." Helena shook her head "We're all in each other's minds now. The three of us”

“What..." They heard Kara try to formulate it just to gulp and run a hand over her eyes like she was seeing a mirage "But...What?"

“No words now?" Helena chuckled towards her "But you were so confident a few seconds ago, demanding answers"

“Helena?" Kara managed to ask when she heard the tone she was so used to from the woman.

“In all my glory"

“But then," Kara looked at the other woman taking up her space "Who are you?"

“An outdated version of me" Helena shrugged and Lena frowned.

“My name is Lena" She tried to explain "Helena and I… We are the same person, more or less" Kara opened her eyes wider if it was possible "Only from different universes"

"Did you deceive me?" Kara asked now getting her attention on her.

Lena watched apprehensively as the blonde tried to process all the information, this was not how she wanted Kara Zor-el to have discovered the truth but in a way, she felt relieved that she didn't have to lie anymore.

When she found out that Kara, her Kara, had lied to her and deceived her for so long she had gone blind with hurt, with resentment. She didn’t want to talk to Kara about it or to try and understand her side, she'd simply assumed the worst; that Supergirl just wanted to use her, just keep her in line. Keep your enemies close, right?

Her time in this universe, despite being short, had taught her that not every lie was due to malice and that sometimes not telling the truth was necessary. Whatever happened going forward, at least, Lena could keep this lesson to herself and she was preparing a wonderful speech about it to try and explain to Kara why tricking her was necessary but it seems like it took too long and Helena had already taken over control of the conversation again.

"Deceive?" Helena huffed "Who are you to talk about lies? About betrayals?"

"I only kissed her because I thought it was you" Kara said exasperated and angry "I'm the victim here"

"Do you think I care about that?" Helena maintained her pose "The betrayal I talk about is much worse than that, it's the betrayal that shows how rotten you are inside"

"Me?” Kara argued "You disappear to who knows where for who knows how long, putting a stranger in your place and I'm the one who has to hear INSULTS"

"Don't raise your voice to me!" Helena advanced towards her and Lena frowned apprehensively not knowing if she should interfere in their conversation.

"But I must! Helena! You put a stranger in command of YOUR planet! I knew you were selfish but not to that extent."

"I didn't put anyone anywhere, do you think I wanted this?" Helena replied angrily "And she's not a stranger, she's me, in a way..."

"Guys... Let's calm down, this conversation is going nowhere, no one intended to deceive anyone..."

"I highly doubt that" Kara looked her in the eye and Lena felt her chest tighten at her gaze.

"We can agree on that" Helena nodded "My wife here is an expert in lies"

"Are you talking about me?" Kara turned back to the Queen's direction and Lena sighed, they both looked like cat and mouse.

"Am I married to someone else?" Helena rolled her eyes "No, unlike you, I stick to my vows of marriage"

"I already said I didn't know…" Kara trailed off "That's beside the point Helena!! The point is, you're selfish!!"

"Coming from you there is no greater irony" She crossed her arms.

"Guys…" Lena tried again but Kara cut her off.

“Why don't you just say what bothers you so much, Helena?” Kara sounded annoyed "All these years I've been putting up with your little side eyes, your careless words... Why don't you grow up and try to solve your problems like the queen you are?”

Lena felt the room freeze and a big crack pass between the three women, even she knew that in that moment Kara had pressed all of Helena's buttons.

“Do you really want to know what bothers me?" The brunette's voice was so low that Lena almost couldn't hear from where she was. "What bothers me is knowing that you made a deal with Rhea behind your father’s back," Kara turned pale and Lena bit her lip, Helena was about to… “What bothers me is that you were the one that let your own father's assassins into the palace, for all I know, may even have been the person who called them in the first place."

"Helena..."

“What bothers you is the fact that I begged you to cancel our wedding, I humiliated myself in front of you and all you told me was that you knew better."

Lena gulped as she heard Helena's side of the story.

“It bothers me that all these years, as you told me yourself, I was watched and cornered because of an unhealthy need for you to know every step of my day!" Kara was white and Helena completely red in contrast "What bothers me is that your lies, your naivety and your plans are the reason my father is dead!"

Silence reigned between the three women in the room, and Lena felt reality shudder a little in response to the strong feelings running through the connection between the three.

“I..." Kara looked shocked by the brunette's confession and Lena could relate. Despite learning everything through Astra’s mouth a few minutes ago, hearing it this way from Helena made everything more real. "Helena, no"

"Are you going to deny it?" She arched an eyebrow in defiance and Kara shook her head.

"I won't deny it" The woman sighed and raised her hands in a gesture of surrender "But you don't know the whole story, your side is distorted, you need to hear me out"

"I don't need to do anything" Helena huffed and turned around, the air getting heavier causing Lena to close her eyes for a moment.

Helena disappeared and appeared again and Kara blinked desperately.

"No! Wait! Listen to me!"

Helena didn't bother to answer before disappearing for good, the air returned to normal and Lena blinked several times seeing the room again become visible. She simply knew that Helena had somehow severed the connection.

She was the kind of woman who would make leave a discussion like that.

“What..." She saw Kara shake her head and look towards where the brunette was for a few seconds "Rao"

Lena knew that as soon as she had a moment to process everything that had happened she would turn against...

"You!" Against her "Bring her back!"

"I don't know how" Lena sighed getting ready for a fight "I didn't have that kind of connection in my Universe"

"Universe..." Kara widened her eyes again "Rao! You're not Helena"

"No" She admitted "My name is Lena Luthor, I'm from Planet Earth" She hesitated "I'm sorry I lied to you but it was necessary"

"Necessary?" Kara huffed "Was it necessary to kiss me too?" The Queen leaned against the doorjamb "It was all a farce..."

"No of course not!" Lena tried to defend herself "I just... I..."

"You know what? That's not even the most important thing now, the most important thing is, how did this happen? How is that possible?"

"I don't know" Lena shook her head in defeat, all this time and she still hadn't figured out the reason, she was getting desperate "One second I was in my universe getting hit by lightning and the next I was waking up here with servants around me and a wife"

Kara glanced at her from the corner of her eye.

"And you don't know how to undo it? How to get her back?"

"No" Lena didn't know how to feel when she saw that Kara wanted to get rid of her soon and have Helena back. How could the blonde prefer insults? "I’m sorry"

Kara just nodded.

"It is true?" Lena asked after hesitating, she didn't want to let the moment escalate and it was her opportunity to hear from the blonde what Astra had said to her "What Helena said?"

"It's complicated…" Kara frowned and looked at her "But it's not you who has to hear the truth, it's Helena! The real one!"

Lena flinched at the blonde's harsh words. Was this how she had treated Kara when she found out the truth? It wasn't a good feeling...

"I understand you're upset right now" Lena tried "But if you want to know everything I can tell you" It was the least the CEO could do.

"Yes" Kara Zor-el nodded "You will tell me everything! Now!"

therapy

"And after all that she still has the audacity to play dumb" Helena huffed, throwing herself as slowly as possible on the big sofa in the living room.

"I understand your frustration Helena, but I think you need to start thinking about it’s coming from"

Helena rolled her eyes and looked at the woman sitting in the chair across from her. She didn't know how she got here, according to the woman, Helena had called as soon as she got back from her connection with Lena and Kara. She still didn't understand how the brunette had managed to make such a strong connection to the point of being visible to a third person, even though the two were touching.

"Spare me the psychoanalysis" She said returning to the present.

"Isn't that why you called me?" Kelly arched an eyebrow in her direction, her question was firm but it didn't sound like a judgment, just an observation.

Helena snorted. Yes, she'd called Alex and requested the psychologist's presence, for some reason she'd had Agent Danvers' comment in mind about her girlfriend being a psychologist and had been the first person who'd thought to speak to.

"Was it?” Helen asked.

"You know Helena, I'm happy to sit with you here for hours and not talk about anything" Kelly shrugged her shoulders "But we both know that if you didn't want help, you wouldn't have called me"

"I don't need help" Now she had gotten defensive.

Kelly stared at her silently.

"Alright then just tell me what happened between you and Lena"

"My problem is not with my other half" She hesitated "Not just with her anyway"

"Who would you say is the source of your problems then?"

"It is not obvious?" The queen was starting to run out of patience, was that what everyone spoke well of on this planet? She could come to that same conclusion in three seconds of thought. "My wife"

"Hm" Kelly didn't give any sign of agreeing or disagreeing with her and that made her sit up straighter.

"I am serious"

"Of course" Kelly said neutrally and Helena felt herself blush, the woman was clearly disagreeing with what she had said.

"I think I would know if there was something else bothering me" Helena scoffed and Kelly smiled.

"May I ask you a rather personal question, Helena?" The psychologist approached the sofa and Helena watched her carefully, she didn't know why she had called her or why she felt that she needed to talk about what had just happened but she always followed her instincts.

"You may” She nodded "I can't guarantee I'll answer"

"When was the last time you thought 'Wow, I’m happy'?" The question was asked kindly but Helena reacted to it just as if it had been shot at.

Happy?

Helena was happy... Occasionally.
She was sure she had several moments where… Well, yes.

She frowned and thought hard about the last few years of her life, when had she ever felt happiness?

"When I finally defeated my mentor Astra" She said confidently "One of the best days of my life"

"Is she still your mentor?"

"No," Helena shook her head lost in her memories "She's one of my generals but I don't want to talk about her"

"Why not?" Kelly studied her curiously.

"Because that's not what I called you for" Helena snorted.

"And why did you call me?"

"Rao, you ask a lot of questions" Helena said in exasperation and got up from the couch, pacing like a caged tiger.

"It's my job" Kelly shrugged but smiled in the brunette's direction. The two enjoyed the silence for a few minutes while Helena continued her movement.

She knew, deep down, why she had called the woman here, someone she didn't even know... Helena was at a dead end, she had finally let out everything that was bothering her to her wife and now she couldn't move forward.

"I told her..." She started trying "I said everything that was bothering me, everything that happened. She didn't deny it"

"And how did that make you feel?" Kelly narrowed her eyes and Helena turned towards her.

"What I feel doesn't matter, only her admission matters"

"Hm" The psychologist muttered again and Helena rolled her eyes.

"I felt relief" The queen admitted and Kelly nodded to herself as if confirming her theory.

"Like a weight has been lifted off your shoulders?"

"Exactly!” Helena sighed and sat back on the couch, calmer now. "What does that mean?"

"What would you like it to mean?"

"What's the point of talking to you if everything we talk about is what I think?" Helena frowned, she couldn't understand this concept of therapy. On Krypton, she was used to having counselors, so she called Kelly when Kara's sister said she’s some kind of version of it, she was used to getting advice but this...

"My job is just to listen to you, guide you..." Kelly shook her head "Your conclusions are yours alone"

"Interesting" Helena squinted at her "So, for example, if I said I think Supergirl is overrated" She watched the woman in front of her "You wouldn't disagree and try to convince me otherwise?"

"No" Kelly admitted "But I would ask you what were the reasons that led you to that conclusion"

"More questions..." Helena huffed "You know… I hate questions"

"Questions are the basis of any healthy conversation" Kelly said seriously "Without them, we're only left with assumptions"

"Are you saying my truths are assumptions?"

"I'm saying an unanswered question is an assumption"

"So..." Helena shook her head "You mean that as long as I don't talk to Kara I won't have...." She trailed off.

"Answers" Kelly completed.

Helena watched the woman in front of her with interest, maybe Earth was onto something with this therapy thing.

"You're good" She admitted and Kelly smiled.

“I know”

Lena knocked on the bedroom door for the third time, she knew the woman was inside and ignoring her.

"What you want?" Astra said crossing her arms "You came here to accuse me of more lies?"

"Kara knows everything" She admitted holding back the urge to cry and watched to see if the woman would let her in or slam the door in her face.

The general seemed to ponder for a few seconds but moves away, indicating for her to enter.

Lena let out a sigh of relief, she didn't know what to do anymore.

"How?" the woman asked as she closed the door.

They started to fight and..." Lena felt like crying "Helena said things... The things you said to me"

The entire weight of the lie and the situation seemed to fall into her lap at once and she sat up in bed. Lena could only remember that in another world, she'd used the same words with her Kara. The tears were falling freely now and she was sure she was crying out loud.

Lena doesn't know how many seconds passed when she felt two arms wrap around her, without any strength to fight she let herself go and calmed down when her head fell on the shoulders of the woman. Astra was comforting her.

"Maybe it's for the best" Astra sighed "Maybe we were wrong..."

"About Kara?"

"And more…" Astra admitted "What did she say when Helena accused her? Did she deny it?"

Lena frowned trying to remember the exact words but she was so nervous she didn't know if she could trust her memory.

"I don't think so" She shook her head "She didn't deny it but she said there was more to the story" Astra turned even paler.

"She told you more?"

Lena shook her head.

Lena swallowed hard.

"For her safety and yours"

intimidation

Helena opened her eyes on a beach under the red Kryptonian sun. It was strange to think that on her home planet, landscapes like this weren't so common and seeing them in Rao's light made everything even more beautiful.

She felt the sand under her feet and knew it was out of her realm. At the same time, something salty in the air made her hair stand on end.

It wasn't Lena waiting for her.

"Helena" She turned around when she heard Kara's voice and lost her breath as she looked into her wife's face after months without seeing her. "Is that really you?"

Years if she counts all the times she didn't want to see her.

"Yes" She sighed without the strength to form a sarcastic reply, her conversation with Kelly had, oddly enough, helped.

The two women stared at each other in silence, with only the soft sound of the ocean waves beside them.

"Is it true what she told me?" Kara broke it "That the switch wasn't on purpose?"

"Lena?" She nodded "It's true"

"How is this possible?" She watched Kara frown in confusion "It shouldn't be possible..."

Helena shrugged and looked at the red horizon.

Would it be less cruel to doubt?

"And have your majesty thought of the matter?"

"Not yet"

Lena felt a shiver run up her spine, something telling her that this man would have been able to do anything to anyone but she didn't have any answers to give him right now.

"Your Majesty?" He approached again and she took a step back. He noticed this time. "Your Highness, I would never do anything to hurt you..."

big promises

Lena knocked softly three times on the door before pushing it open. She was tired but knew she needed to have this conversation. She and Astra waited for Kara to look for them for hours before giving up and retreating.

Not that she gave up, Lena couldn't sleep a wink all night, torn between looking for Kara, trying to talk to Helena, or getting some rest.

"Ruby?" But now she had something more important to do...she couldn't let the girl run away from the conversation they had to have any longer, "Ruby, I need to talk to you..."

"If Your Majesty doesn't mind, I'd rather be alone" The teenager said very dryly from where she was in the bedroom lounge. She was reading some book and had gotten up when she heard the Queen's voice.

"Ruby..." Lena sighed, she knew the girl was trying to pull away "May we please talk? You have every right to be upset with me"

"No, Your Majesty" The girl shook her head "I have no right, Your Majesty can do what you want, it's your right"

You're starting to sound like someone who cares." Kara smiled trying to change the mood in the training room. Helena analyzed the woman in front of her and all her behavior since she appeared in this universe. Although all her troubled past with both Karas, this one in particular had never required anything but respect and Helena could see merit in it.

She’s just starting to separated the two women in her mind, separated her actions and her judgments. Maybe it was time to separate them in a definite way and what better than accepting it in her life?

“If you insist" Helena pretended to be angry about having to be respectful "as long as you and your sister don’t test my patience"

They wouldn’t last one day.

forgiveness

"Kara!" Lena called entering the lighthouse. She knew the blonde was hiding in here somewhere. "Kara?"

It’s been two days since they'd returned to the capital and despite all the plans Lena’d made to talk to the blonde alone about everything that had happened, the woman had been avoiding her for the entire journey back to the palace.

It was easy to get lost in the palace, she knew that in addition to the plethora of duties and meetings the two of them had to attend, there was the fact that their quarters were in opposite wings, making it nearly impossible for Lena to accidentally bump into Queen Zor-el…

She didn't even know if Kara was still mad at her about impersonating Helena for so long, about kissing her...

At least she hadn't been accused of any crime.
Yet.

"Do you trust me?" Lena countered with her own question, trying to buy time to think about the answer she would have to give. Did she trust Kara? If it were Kara Danvers she would say yes without a second thought, even after all the lies between them but this Kara? With all the secrets and manipulations behind her crown?

Lena couldn't say.

On the one hand, she wanted to trust the blonde, wanted to believe in her words and in everything the two had lived so far. Lena knows that everyone can make mistakes and that the actions that Helena ponders so much may in fact be just that, mistakes. At the same time, Lena finds herself being pulled by the evidence and narratives of Astra and Helena herself, whom have lived much longer with Kara Zor-el than Lena.

It was a battle between mind and feelings.

"I did" Kara admitted looking at her with an unreadable expression.

"I don't know what to tell you" Lena crossed her arms "I want to trust you but after everything Helena told me..."

"Helena..." Kara huffed and rolled her eyes at her wife's name "Helena has a distorted view of me and honestly Lena? I don't think I can fix this anymore"

To the brunette's surprise, Lena saw a small tear slip discreetly down the blonde's cheek.

"Maybe if you guys talk about everything..." Lena tried to help.

"We did” Kara admitted "She told me she doesn't want to be my wife anymore"

"I'm sorry" Lena frowned in rebuke to her alternate version. She doesn’t think she'd made the best choice in telling the woman that.

"That's beside the point now" The blonde shrugged her shoulders "I don't even know why I'm telling you this, I shouldn't even be talking to you"

"Kara"

"I just want to understand why so many lies" She shook her head.

"I told you..." Lena sighed looking down at her "I didn't know who to trust"

"And you trusted my aunt?" She questioned intrigued "Why her and not me? I could have helped, I could have done something..."

"I didn't tell Astra anything" Lena denied remembering the meeting between the two in the red room "She found out I wasn't Helena in our first conversation"

Kara was silent after that.

"What should I think? You were her wife and couldn't tell us apart while she could see that I wasn't Helena in seconds. It was obvious who was the closest person to her... It was obvious that I should trust Astra"

"Are you saying my aunt said I couldn't be trusted?" Kara narrowed her eyes "Did she tell you to keep it a secret?"

"Yes" Lena swallowed but confirmed, that's what had happened. "She said it was imperative to keep the peace on Krypton that no one knew about the switch, not even you."

"She’s not wrong about not telling everyone. Rhea would definitely take advantage of that to attack us" Kara looked at her sideways "It's one of the reasons I'll keep her secret" Lena sighed in relief "But I'll never understand how she - how you - could keep it from me"

"I'm sorry, Kara" was all Lena could offer as an explanation. The reason, in fact, had already been said. "I like you very much" She admitted after hesitating "I wouldn't want you to be angry at me"

"I'm not angry" She said grudgingly "I just feel... wronged"

Lena closed her eyes. She could understand that feeling all too well and it ached in her soul that she had been the cause of it in someone else but she had no choice.

No, she had a choice, she just chose wrong.

"I understand" Lena thought twice before telling her story but knew that if she was going to try to trust the Queen in front of her, she would have to be brave and put herself in that position "On Earth, in my universe, you - I mean - my Kara lied to me too" Kara looked at her with interest "She deceived me for a long time and when I found out, well... For a long time I couldn't forgive her for that. I couldn't understand her motives and kept wondering what I could have done differently to win her trust or her friendship because in my head, her friendship wasn't true"

"If she lied to you, it couldn't have been.” Kara completed but Lena shook her head.

"But it was" She denied "Her lie had nothing to do with me, it wasn’t because of me... Her hesitation and her fear of telling me weren't my fault. She’d traumas, she was trapped in circumstances where the truth would hurt more than the lie and although I don't agree with her reasons, today, after everything I've lived here with you, I can understand her decisions"

"So you forgave her?" Kara asked with genuine curiosity.

"Yes, I forgave her" Lena said for the first time "And I would like the opportunity to tell her that, if I ever go back"

"You'll go back, believe me, when Helena puts her mind to something she always manages to do it and she seemed to be very concerned about making this Mxy guy undo the switch" Kara said without much emotion in her voice but Lena could see that her posture of defensiveness had been lowered after her words. Wait, Mxy?

"Mxy?" Lena frowned, why hadn't Helena talked to this about her? Had she found a way to make the two of them switch places again? "What do you mean undo the switch?"

"She didn't tell you?" Kara looked at her confused and Lena blinked and denied "She told me he was the one who made the switch, a being from the fifth dimension... You know, these beings were considered mythology on this side of the universe. Clearly things are quite different from where you’re from..."

Lena wasn't paying much attention to what Kara was saying after that, she finally had a clue. She had an answer...

So it hadn't been an accident or an electrical storm that had caused her to travel across the universe and occupy the body of another version of herself. No, someone had done it.

This explanation raised more questions than it answered them, the main one being: why? What does this being gain from her switch with Helena? And why hadn't she told her about it?

Lena sighed, things were happening too fast and she couldn't afford to alienate another version of her, she was going to need to talk to her as soon as possible.

The chance to return to her universe was so close that Lena could feel the electricity running through her fingers. In a way, Lena was getting used to being in this universe because she didn't have the slightest idea how to get back to hers, but now that that possibility exists she feels a tingle in her stomach.

Whether it was good thing or bad thing she doesn’t know.

the white room

Chapter Notes

TW: From this point on, this story will discuss sexual abuse and mental and physical torture. Please read with caution.

Lena woke up as if waking up from a deep coma. Her senses didn't seem to return and she was slow to blink. At first, the light hurt her eyes, and she closed them again.

A strong smell of metal penetrated her nostrils and she wrinkled her nose in response. What is this? She opened her eyes again and her heart skipped a beat when she saw four white walls surrounding her.

Lena is not in her quarters.
Where she was?
She remembers saying goodbye to Kara at the lighthouse, going down the stairs - yes, she didn't use the elevator - having dinner in her room and finally sleeping in her bed, well, Helena's bed.
How was she here?

More important than that... Where exactly was here?

Frowning, she tried to lift her body to get a better look at the white room where she'd woken up, only to feel herself being pulled back into bed. Looking down, she sees that her torso is held together by some sort of leather ribbon. Shaking her hands, she feels a twinge of pain to see that they were leather-bound to the bed.

The situation reminded her of mental hospitals back on Earth.

Is that what this was?
Had she gone crazy?

Maybe Lena hadn't switched worlds, maybe she had a psychotic break and got hospitalized? Maybe Helena didn't exist...

"What the hell is this?" Speaking of her...

"Helena!" Lena turned her head in the direction of the voice and watched as her counterpart materialized beside her. "Are you real?"

"No" Helena said approaching the bed "I can't interfere with your reality"

Looking in Helena's direction, she could see that the Queen was no better than her. The woman was frozen staring at Rhea and only moved when she could see the woman wasn't seeing her.

Suddenly, a lot of her behavior made sense.
A lot of it.

She wouldn't put up with it like Helena had.
She had to get out of here.

"You and your planet will be mine, we both know that, so don’t fight it”

She pulled away and Lena released the breath she'd been holding.

"How long do you think your wife will sit at the negotiating table this time?" She laughed and Lena closed her eyes. Kara! The two Karas... If they only knew what Rhea had done to Helena... What she planned to do to her... “You and I had a lot of fun while we talked to her, remember?" She ran her hand over Lena's torso.

She saw her other self trembling and Lena looked away from Helena. She couldn't face the woman.
Unfortunately, her eyes met Rhea's.

She looked at her with glee and Lena wanted to scream.

"There you are. I knew you'd come back to me... Do you remember that time I had you with your wife's hologram in the room?" She laughed in the direction of the guards "Of course she couldn't see anything but I could... I’ve thought a lot about that moment since you left me"

Lena couldn't believe what she was hearing.
She didn't know who she was more horrified for… Helena who had lived through it all or herself, who was about to.

"I seen that you've grown more stubborn since the last time we saw each other" Rhea pulled away even further "But don't worry, you'll be back to normal soon. Think a lot about me... Boys, take care of my darling girl”

The wall opened again and Rhea walked out of the room. With it, the bonds that seemed to have stuck Helena. The woman finally stirred, approaching Lena.

"Don't say anything" Her voice was like ice and Lena wanted to scream in her direction. She wanted to scream... How did you not tell anyone about this? How did you endure? How can I…?

Lena just nodded.

"They'll beat you a little" Lena saw the guards untying her feet "Don't fight, the more you fight, the more they'll beat you. Believe me, I know"

Lena didn't move her feet.

"I killed the first five guards she sent me" Lena wanted to ask how but she couldn't talk to Helena while they were in the room. They couldn't know the one thing that could save her. "She just sent more. And stronger..."

They untied her torso.

"You need to be strong Lena. I promise I will find a way to get you out of here but you need to be strong and hold on. I won’t let you go through I went through”

"Don't talk" Helena said quickly cutting her off. Luckily the guards were too busy getting her out of bed to notice.

Sick

Chapter Notes

TW

Helena woke up with a heavy breath and her hand on her chest. Her breathing was labored and she’d feel the satin sheets gripping her back. Everything was wrong, it hurt her and her body screamed like she'd just come out of Rhea's captivity.

Rhea...
She shivered.

She’d never told anyone about the worst part of the torture she had endured at the hands of the Queen of Daxam. Helena had pushed those memories so deeply that reliving them in the short minutes she'd been in her presence had been torture and, like the first time, Helena found herself paralyzed.

She tasted guilt on her lips.

Rationally, she knew she wasn't to blame for the abuse she suffered, but her body still punished her every time she thought about it.

Why hadn't she fought when it was all she'd ever done before?

She fought against the guards, against her wife, against the generals... against everyone!
Everyone but Rhea.
When the woman touched her like that...

Helena vomited onto the side of the bed.
Her stomach churned as she poured everything out. She breathed hard for a few seconds, great, now she’d made a mess.

Getting up on the other side of the bed, avoiding the vomit, Helena tried to balance herself. She needed to recover, and fast! Lena needed her.

Swallowing her fears and her reactions, Helena went to the suite's bathroom. Not having the courage to look in the mirror, she washed her face and drank the water from the sink, getting the bad taste out of her mouth. She braced her hands on the marble and lowered it. She couldn't afford to have a nervous breakdown right now.

A flashing noise startled her and she jumped from her position, her hand searching for a weapon that wasn't there. In her nervousness about getting out of bed, she hadn't brought with her the knife she always kept beside her and was now too far from the closet to fetch the gun she kept there. Looking around quickly, she saw nothing she could use to defend herself and took a deep breath. She would have to trust her training.

Her training had failed her before... Not even the best fighter could fight triple the number of opponents.

Or with Rhea.
She couldn't fight Rhea.
It’s her weakness.

Taking a deep breath, Helena left the bathroom expecting to meet the woman who haunts her nightmares. If she was thinking rationally she would know there was no way she could be here, in this reality.

But Helena wasn't being rational right now.

"Helena?" She heard Kara Danvers' voice coming from some dark corner of the room "Are you ok? Are you feeling sick?"

Helena advanced into the room and turned on the light, looking at Supergirl at the entrance to her bench.

"Do you feel sick?" She asked again looking at the state Helena had left the room in. She would be embarrassed in any other situation. Now she had more important things to worry about. "Do you want to go to the hospital?"

"No" She said desperately and Kara looked worried, having never seen the brunette like that "It's Lena... Rhea has her! We need to help her"

She said it all at once as if it was the last chance she would ever have to speak. She couldn't fail.

She couldn't fail again.

"Rao!" Kara started toward her, but Helena flinched. She moved as far away as she could within the room's distance. She couldn't bear to be touched right now "Are you okay? Is she okay?"

"No" She bit her lip. If she focused enough, she could feel the pain in her rib Lena was feeling right now from the punches she'd received from Rhea's guards. "We need to be quick, soon Rhea will get tired of her games and leave for..." She cut herself off.

How to explain?
How could she even begin to tell her everything Rhea was planning?

"Helena, I don't know what we can do" Now it was Kara who was starting to get desperate. It was clear from her gaze that her not being able to help was killing her inside.

"We have to go there" Helena said firmly and without a doubt, she knew what they needed. "We have to be..."

"Myxlplyx!" Kara said aloud and with a snap, the fifth dimensional goblin appeared in the room.

"What the hell happened here?" He frowned at the state of the place "Are you sick?" He looked in Helena's direction.

"You bastard goblin" Helena advanced towards, little red dots in her vision "Take me back now!"

He disappeared just as she was going to hold him.

"Rude!" He appeared on top of the bed "I already said that the change can only be un..."

"Mxy! Lena is in danger" Kara interrupted him. "We need to help her”

He paused whatever he was going to say.

"What do you mean in danger?"

"Rhea" Helena almost spat her name "I don't know how, but she has her. She's with Lena"

"Well... That's..." He scratched the back of his head.

"Mxy! Undo the trade now" Kara ordered and Helena's heart skipped a beat.

Undoing the exchange…
That would take her straight into Rhea's arms.
She visible shuddered.

She couldn't think about that.
She needed to be in her own universe.
She needed to protect Lena.
The woman didn't deserve what Rhea had planned for Helena. It was Helena who ran away, it was Helena who made her angry.

Helena should be the one to pay for her crimes, not Lena.

"You see..." He hesitated "I can't undo the switch"

She snarled at him and he jumped in fear.
"Easy, easy" he raised his hands "Like I said... The exchange will undo itself, when Lena and Helena are ready"

"What do you mean when they’re ready?" Kara asked nervously "She needs us now!! She's in danger!"

"I don't make the rules Supergirl" He said shrugging his shoulders "I sincerely hope everything works out but I can't help you”

And just as she appeared, he disappeared.
Helena swallowed a scream of rage.

"What will we do now?" Kara asked desperately, already pulling out a cell phone no doubt to talk to Alex.

Helena just resigned herself to her fate.
She would have to talk to Kara!
Her Kara.

"I need to talk to my wife"

She just hoped that this time, Kara Zor-El would do the right thing.

crossroads

Chapter Notes

TW: mentions of sexual abuse

Helena entered her wife's chambers for what had to be the first time in years, if not ever.

She doesn’t remember being here before and the decor caught her eye. Contrary to what she thought, the woman's taste didn't make her sick, the leather is well mixed with shades of black and the red details were inviting.

Of course, this could all just be an illusion, after all, Helena was in Kara Zor-El's subconscious and this room could just be in her mind.

Looking for her wife, she saw her lying in bed, completely at peace and asleep. For a split second, Helena felt anger rise in her throat, even in the depths of her mind, the blonde had peace while Helena's mind is her own private hell.

As she got closer, however, she saw that things were not what they seemed.

For starters, the Queen of Krypton was tied to her bed by golden threads, motionless as thick tears streamed down her face. She was trapped in some nightmare, so deep that even Helena with all her mental skills couldn't access it.

Her only solution was to 'wake her up'

"Kara!" She called out without touching her and saw the blonde thrashing around on the bed in response. With no time to waste, Helena shook the woman. "Wake up!"

Kara opened her eyes and sucked in air hard, her body breaking free of the golden bonds and looking towards her.

"Lena? How did she get into my room?"

Helena would be offended by the confusion if she wasn't happy that the woman had emerged from the depths of her nightmare.

"I am not Lena" She said as Kara composed herself "And I'm not in your room, not really"

"Helena?" The blonde narrowed her eyes and looked around. "Are you in my mind?"

Helena just rolled her eyes and walked away.

The proximity to her wife made her nervous, especially after their last conversation. Blaming Zor-el was all Helena knows and without that hatred, she doesn’t know what to feel or how to behave.

But that wasn't the point.
The important thing was Lena Luthor under the clutches of her enemy.

"Rhea has Lena" She said directly before the connection could be weakened, she knew her wife had mental connection’s training, but also knew that this was not her strongest suit.

"Rhea?" she asked with fear in her voice and for the first time, Helena didn't criticize her for it.

"Yes!”

"But...but how? I was talking to her just yesterday? How did Rhea get in here? How..."

The scenery quickly changed, causing a twinge in her head. Now the two found themselves at a crossroads, with four paths around them.

"Subtle" Helena looked around.

"Helena!!" Kara called her back to the present, she didn't seem to have noticed the change of scenery. "How could this have happened? How do you know that?"
