"You don't understand" The brunette shook her head and the roads around her shook.

"So explain it to me" Kara approached her wife, slowly enough for Helena to move away from her if she wanted to. Placing her hands on her shoulders, their eyes meet. "What did she do to you?"

Kara felt Helena stiffen in her arms.

"It would be easier to ask what she didn't do" The brunette admitted and the queen exhaled.

Suddenly, Kara Zor-el was no longer in the mood for negotiations.

"I'll deal with her" she promised seriously, her eyes shining with unspoken words "She'll pay, Helena"

All the roads around her became one. Kara hadn't noticed, but Helena knew she'd made a decision.

The brunette relaxed in her arms.

"I don't know why I'm trusting you again" she shook her head "But I hope I'm not wrong"

"You’re not" the blonde nodded "What do you want me to do first?"

Astra was in the midst of training the new recruits when the doors to the red room burst open. The general watched as her Queen and niece, Kara Zor-el entered the room followed by three of Helena's generals, well, now they're Lena's, of course.

Astra was trying to adapt to the changing realities between her Queen Helena and a plain, pale version of her. Confused and lost like a little child.

If she'd wanted to, she could have brought this Lena person down on her first day here but no, she couldn't. Astra loved Helena Kieran Zor-el as if the woman were her own daughter and that alone prevented her from doing everything that should be done.

Including dealing with her killer of a niece.

Whom at this moment was coming towards her with a small army at her back.

That doesn’t look good.

"Aunt Astra" She greeted her.

"My Queen" She looked around, the new recruits distancing themselves from the two of them in awe. "To what do I owe the honor?"

"Rhea" She said and Astra froze, her eyes quickly roaming the room looking for a way out but she knew in the back of her mind that there was none, not against the small army Zor-el had brought with her.

Astra was good, but she was only one.

Was this how the great General Astra would die? Betrayed by her own family?

Rhea had managed to win after all.

No, she had to fight.
Helena needed her and Rao, Lena needed her too. She couldn't leave the poor child in her niece's hands.

"You bring shame on your country and your family, Kara Zor-el" Astra tried to buy time "Selling herself like that to your own enemy"

Astra frowned at the mention of Helena.
Why would Kara talk about her if she already knew about the change? Had the queen been in contact with her wife?

If her niece was going to kill her, they should stay. Surely she knew that in a fight between the two of them, Astra would win?

Theodore, Kiliot and Hunter left without a word. Their little army and the new recruits with them.

No trace of how Rhea could have captured her" Astra could see that her niece was frustrated "I've already started to call all the generals as you can see but, Aunt, I need your help"

Instinct

Lena felt herself wince in pain as she tried to roll over in the bed. No, how many times would she turn to her left side forgetting that was where they'd hit her the most?

Grunting, she tried the other side. Yes, this one was better. How long was she here? Better yet... How long would she stay here? Lena didn't know.

She’d already lost track of time inside that white room. If Rhea's goal was to drive her crazy, the brunette had to admit, it was almost working.

She was fighting with all her might to stay sane as guards and more guards rotated to see who could hit her the hardest. She wasn't like Helena, she couldn't keep herself awake for half the time the other version of her had, that they were expecting her to… No, she passed out much faster than the real Queen of Krypton.

At least with that the torture ends faster. After she passes out, the guards usually leave her alone for awhile... The first time, she even had been attended to by a Daxamite doctor.

Her only solace was that Rhea had left her alone so far. When she'd seen the panic on Helena's face at the Queen of Daxam's insinuation, Lena had panicked herself thinking about what Rhea would do to her. But so far, the woman had not kept her promises.

Small mercies, she would say.

She only hoped that Helena had kept her promise and that she had done something to warn Kara of her capture.

What would Kara do though?

"Wow, that's weird"

Kara!

Her Kara! Kara Danvers!

I promise I will fight her" She approached " Are you okay? She did anything to you?"

"No Lena, please don't cry" Kara looked at her desperately "I promise we will help you ok? Helena is talking to the Kara from her universe, she will help you ok? I'm trying to figure everything with the Myx too, I promise..."

"Lena, please look at me..."

She couldn't.
Couldn't.

She was crossing the line.

What has changed?
What has she done differently? Oh...

About her Kara.
Instinctively, when she was in pain, she called for Kara Danvers in her mind.

What does that mean?

Whatever plans Rhea had for her, Lena wouldn't give her the satisfaction of seeing her break.

realization

Lena ran her hand over the fabric covering her body with regret. She knew she’d never be able to wear that shade of red again, the long dress barred at her feet made it look like she was levitating through the corridors of the spaceship Rhea was holding her prisoner.

Two guards were leading her down the hall, pulling her arm in such a way that it was hurting her, but she no longer worried about the pain. All Lena could think about now was how to keep herself safe enough until Kara could rescue her.

Or until she could somehow rescue herself.

Turning left into a long hallway, Lena saw a figure leaning against the wall. Just by her posture she could tell it was Helena. She didn't know whether to feel comforted or anxious by the brunette's presence. No, Lena actually knew, the woman's presence was like a balm, like a lifeboat to the situation Lena found herself in.

"Kara wanted to come to you again, but the connection is unstable with two people and I thought you’d need me... At least more than you would need her right now" Helena whispered in the hallway. Lena just nodded in her direction and kept walking, she knew the guards couldn't hear the woman but she couldn't respond for fear they'd realize something was wrong.

However, all Lena wanted to tell her was that Helena didn't need to be here… She didn't need to see and hear whatever Rhea wanted to do to her. Lena didn't want the queen to relive her trauma but at the same time, she knew she wanted company right now. Lena was conflicted.

Finally the hallway ended at a large door that led to a sort of hall. Lena was led by the two guards to the Hall, Helena followed her silently, not that anyone could hear her footsteps, but perhaps because of the seriousness of the situation. The walls of the hall were covered in scriptures she couldn't understand, in some language her brain couldn't translate.

Both Lena and Helena let out a breath filled with relief when they saw that the Hall was packed with people and soldiers to be more precise. Relief quickly gave way to worry, it looked like Rhea was getting ready for a war.

And soon it became clear the reason for so much concern, ahead Lena could see two great thrones, one empty and the other occupied by the queen of Daxam. One of the guards forced Lena to sit on the other throne and when the brunette turned around she could see the Machiavellian smile on the queen's face which made her curious to know where the woman was looking. Turning her head slightly, Lena understood the whole reason for concern.

Ahead of them was Kara Zor-el, in all her Kryptonian queen splendour. The woman also seemed to be prepared for battle, her expression was closed and she wore her war uniform, a blue and red armor. Though her eyes didn't leave Rhea for a second, Lena knew the blonde had raked her gaze over her body just from the shiver she felt down her spine.

Kara disguised it well and didn't give the queen of Daxam the satisfaction of seeing her worried.

Usually, her doppelganger's presence in her mind was like a big beacon behind her eyes, or like someone was constantly hammering her brain. Ever since she's seen her in the hallways, however, it's like Lena barely feels her near.

This hall will turn into a big cauldron of blood and everyone will try to use you as a shield." Lena looked around and saw that some guards were approaching slowly to where she was sitting. Feel annoyed about caring about someone.

Love?

weakness

Helena straightened her posture the moment she heard the big doors slam and the sound of gunfire and bombs rippling through the hall. Suddenly everything around her calmed down. She’s in her element and her hand itches for a weapon.

Her years of training gave her the upper hand in such situation. Helena gets into a situation and immediately analyzes it: Who is the commander? How dangerous is this place? Where are the exits? Who was she to protect and how many people can she kills before she dies? These are the basic questions her brain asks itself when it finds itself in a dangerous situation.

She analyzed the place, it was open enough to get lost in the chaos and if Helena was in control of her body, she would kill the guard to her right who was distracted for a few seconds due to the Kryptonian army entering, pull him through his back a little and twist his neck, holding him because that would make him fall. Helena would take his weapon, she preferred to fight with a sword but you don't take a knife to a gun fight, and she would go unnoticed between shots, killing discreetly when she needed to.

Unfortunately, it wasn't Helena who was in control of her body at the moment, and despite having—much grudgingly—admitted that Lena Luthor would do whatever it took, she knew the woman didn't have the training she did.

She couldn't risk her life...

Helena hated that she’d begun to care about this alternate version of herself.

"Get behind the throne" She muttered quickly. All of her calculations didn't last even ten seconds and she’s already ruled out all other alternatives. Lena only had one option.

She had no choice.

 

"Mike" She whispered and Astra rushed to her side.

 

The general hugged her from the side and Helena threw herself into her arms. She didn't cry but was grateful for the comfort that the older one gave her.

 

Mike.

Billy or Mike?

Good or bad?

Bad.

 

She wasn't well and she trusted Astra more than she trusted her own mother.

 

"I froze" Helena confessed and Astra pulled back to look into her eyes "I thought I would be able to confront her. Able to..." Helena shook her head "But I couldn't move. I couldn't speak. If Lena hadn’t been in my body..." She shuddered "Rhea would have killed me"

 

"Helena... None of this is your fault" Astra said "It’s your body's normal reaction. She hurt you a lot."

 

"I'm a war general, Astra" Helena let go of the woman, reaching the limit of physical contact with another person. "I'm a queen, for Rao's sake. And I stood there like a child."

 

"It's not your fault"

 

"Then whose is it?" Helena laughed disparagingly "I only have myself to blame"

 

"And me..." She confessed and Helena stopped breathing for a second. "Helena, I need your forgiveness"

 

"My forgiveness?” She frowned and involuntarily covered herself with the red dress again. "What did you do?" She looked up.

 

"I... I let my doubts poison me" She shook her head and walked away "And then I poisoned you"

 

Helena looked at her.

 

"Against Kara"

 

She let out her breath.

She’d already imagined...

 

"I really thought things were black and white... I thought she was... Anyway, it doesn't matter what I thought" She continued "I just know that my niece did everything in the last few days to rescue Lena. Rescue you! And prove to you that she was serious... Prove that..."

 

"Stop” Helena almost whispered.

She didn't want to have this conversation with her general. "Astra, please..."

 

"Helena, don't do this" The older woman smiled gently "I know you like a mother knows a daughter that came out of her womb. You are my daughter in everything but blood" She saw her eyes glistening with tears that she would never let go fall "And in the effort to protect you I ended up hurting you... And hurting my own blood too"

 

"Astra, the things she did..." Helena tried not to surrender to the words of the woman she trusted. "Things... don't change because her intentions were good. The end is always the same: people are dead and she is responsible"

 

"I don't think so…” Astra shook her head in denial "I've had a lot of time to think about everything we've been through, Helena" She rubbed a hand over her face in despair "Ever since the other version of you came into our world... I don't stop thinking and... Yes, Helena! Intentions count"

 

"No" Helena couldn't.

Not after all these years.

 

"Yes" Astra whispered gently. "Forgive me, but yes"

 

Helena swallowed hard.

 

She'd already lost count of how long she'd waited to hear that. How long had she prayed and screamed and begged for an explanation. For a reason.

 

An excuse.

 

How long had she wanted even a crumb to let Kara into her life again? How long had she stared at herself in the mirror without recognizing the woman she had become because of her...

 

The day when...

The day she had seen Kara's biological signature in the matrix, confirming what Astra had told her, was the day something broke inside her.

 

When Helena buried her father six feet underground and looked around for...

 

No.

She didn't accept that explanation now.

Now it was too late.

 

Helena has always been afraid of falling in love.

Because that was the reason for the problem wasn't it? Astra knew... And that's why she was looking at her with a compassion in that moment, a compassion that Helena wanted to make her swallow with punches and blood.

 

Yes, that was the reason.

 

Helena has always been of the world but at some point, years ago, before the world hit her with its black holes, for a second... She was Kara's.

 

Helena placed her hand on her chest, trying to hold her heart.

 

"Helena?” Astra was worried, her breathing quickened.

 

The Queen just shook her head.

 

It wasn't fair that Astra gave her permission now. Now!

 

Helena has always run towards danger, since she was a child. Without fear, she threw herself from chasms and drowned in emotions. Facing your enemies, like this, eye to eye and without fear of repeating the dose.

 

Except Kara.

 

No, she was always different...

The sweet daughter of the King who threw glances and flirted and was kind and...

 

For a moment in the past, Helena had looked at that girl with reverence. And although many times, feeling irritated by her innocence, Helena remembers noticing the tiny change in her voice.

 

She was amazed at how much attention Kara stole from her.

 

For years she was astonished.

And she couldn't forgive what she did, she couldn't!

 

She couldn't let her father down, killed because of her.

She couldn't let down her subjects, locked in a war because of her.

And she couldn't disappoint Astra, who had betrayed her own blood to protect Helena.

 

What was left if she didn't hate her?

 

Helena blinked in shock.

 

For years, hating Kara was the only way out of the hell she was living. From the hell that it was, looking at all sides of that palace and seeing the history between the two bleeding on the walls.

 

Seeing the birth of a feeling taken by the pain of betrayal.

 

And to her end, for years, Helena convinced herself that she no longer thought about her wife. But whenever possible she did not fail to remind herself that she had already forgotten her.

 

It was an endless cycle.

A cycle that Helena had never given herself permission to break and now...

 

Now Astra had broken the glass case Helena had kept her beliefs. She had pulled her into the light and given her permission to... For what?

 

She laughed in despair.

 

"And what do you want me to do, Astra?" She felt her eyes burn "Everything... Everything that happened was not in vain and Kara is not innocent"

 

"But she's not guilty either"

The last lock opened on Helena's armor and she even felt her posture relax.

 

"I'm not telling you to run into her arms and kiss the woman” Astra said gently "But maybe... maybe it's time for you to forgive her. I remember a time when her innocence was something that you admired"

 

"That was many years ago, Astra" She didn't say she was thinking about it too "We were different people"

 

"Helena... Just think about it, okay? You know that..."

 

The two were interrupted by the sound of the door opening. It was the doctor coming back to finish his exams.

 

"I no longer wish to discuss this matter" Helena said with finality. She'd already heard everything she needed to hear from Astra.

 

If she would hear from Kara...

And if Kara would hear from her...

That was another story.

 

"Billy or Mike, Helena?" She repeated the question and this time Helena hesitated, looking at her mentor.

 

"Billy"

who else to trust if not yourself

"No, no, no" Helena repeated as she opened her eyes on the roof of Lena Luthor's office, her other version.

"Helena?" She heard her calling and turned quickly. Without feeling pain, she understood that she was in Lena's mind.

The queen sighed in relief and found herself thinking, she was grateful to be in her body again.

"Lena Luthor" She looked at the woman - now in her true body - it was easy to see the differences between the two. One had to wonder how Lena had managed to deceive so many people. Even her posture was different "It's nice to finally meet you properly"

Lena snorted and laughed.

"I don't know if I can say the same" She arched an eyebrow and Helena rolled her eyes. "How are you?" She asked "I remember passing out before I came back"

"Yes" Helena leaned against the frame of the penthouse, her posture relaxed, something she couldn't do in the bed she was being forced to stay in right now "A few broken ribs and several bruises.”

"I'm sorry" Lena whispered "Should I have... I don't know, fought harder?"

Helena sighed and looked at her.
She shouldn't feel guilty.
Not even a little bit.

"No, Lena" Helena said seriously "Don't be sorry... If it wasn't for you... I froze" She repeated what she had said to Astra.

"That's normal after what she did to you, Helena"

Helena nodded but didn't answer. She was still trying to understand her reaction and forgive herself.

"And Kara?" Lena asked after a few seconds, sounding shy. Was she afraid Helena would scold her for asking? "How is she? I didn't have time to say goodbye..."

Helena shrugged.
She didn't know if she wanted to discuss her wife with her other self.
A version that clearly liked her-if her kisses counted for anything.

"Helena?” She insisted "Is she okay?"

"She is"

Thunder rumbled in the distance.

"You don’t want to talk about Kara?"

Helena denied, shaking her head.
Her entire body refused to even think about the woman, yet her mind didn't seem to agree and before she knew it, her mouth had opened.

"She doesn't even know I'm back"

"You didn’t tell her?" Lena asked in shock and suddenly thanked her Kara for recognizing her so quickly.

"No"

Lena sat down, trying not to look intimidating to the woman. Clearly, Helena was trying to find answers within herself.

"And is there a reason?" she asked gently.

"Perhaps..."

"Helena, you are not a child"

The queen rolled her eyes.

"Yes, there is" She confessed "There are several reasons... Fear, shame, anger! Feelings!"

"You feel angry and I understand, Helena..." Lena frowned "But fear? Or shame? I should feel ashamed, not you! None of this is your fault"

"But it's…" She laughed sheepishly. "I talked to Astra"

Lena tried to follow the reasoning of the other version of her but it was like following a soap opera with the chapters cut. She couldn't understand the whole story and Helena kept skipping from parts to parts. Maybe it was her way of processing… God knows Lena Luthor didn't process trauma the way the rest of the world did either.

"And what did she said?"

"That she was wrong to judge Kara so quickly... That her intentions were good... Ha! What a joke" She snorted.

Lena narrowed her eyes.
"I don't think you think that's a joke, Helena" her tone wasn't judgmental "If not, you wouldn't even think about it! What did Astra tell you that got you so upset?"

Helena felt rain wet her face. Large drops ran down her cheek as if the sky was crying for her. Crying over what Helena refused to accept.

"Why…" She hesitated and fell silent.

When Helena didn't continue, Lena sighed and insisted "Helena, if you can't trust yourself... Who are you going to count on?"

Helena squinted at her.
Several points could be discussed there... First, she didn't consider herself the same person as Lena. And her circumstances made trust hard to earn. On the other hand, Luthor was right.

And besides...
Who would Lena tell?
She was no longer in her body, had no way of communicating... Her eyes widened.

"Wait... How are you talking to me? How did I connect with you?"

Lena frowned.
"Like always?"

Helena snorted.
Why was the other version of her so slow?

"No! Lena! We're not switched anymore!" She exclaimed "This shouldn't be possible anymore"

"I don't understand" Lena stepped closer.

"We had a connection because you were using my body" Helena tried to be patient "But now... That shouldn't be possible"

"Are you saying that the two of us are still linked somehow?"

Helena nodded.
"Yes" she sighed "That's exactly what I'm saying"

Suddenly everything went white and a flash of light blinded her. She blinked once, twice…her eyes scratched at each other until she got used to it.

"Why isn't she waking up?" She heard someone - Was it Kara? ask.

"She's already waking up, Your Majesty! Don't worry" Another voice replied "Your Highness, don't rush, open your eyes slowly”

"What...?" Helena was confused. Where was she?

She must have asked aloud because the voice answered.

"Your Majesty is in the medical wing of the Vox, spaceship of the Kryptonian army. We are almost home, Your Highness"

Helena blinked and her eyes adjusted to the light. A doctor she remembered catching a glimpse of a few hours ago giving her a sleeping pill was on her right side. On her left side - with a small group due to her concussion - was her wife.

"I'll go get your next medicine, Your Majesty! Please try not to move too much"

Helena was going to nod but her head was hurting too much for any movement.

"Hey"
She heard Kara Zor-el whisper beside her and Helena swallowed hard.

Her entire conversation with Astra flashed through her mind.

"Hey"
She managed to answer.

"We need to talk" Her wife said firmly but kindly and Helena knew she couldn't escape the conversation they should have had on their wedding day. Or even before...

She bit her lip, a sign of nervousness unusual for the queen.

"So let's talk"

What’s in a name?

Helena sat up in bed, adjusting her posture. Her wife sounded serious and the brunette had doubts if her identity was still safe.

 

Suddenly, she wanted to be recognized.

She wanted Kara Zor-el to know who she was talking to.

 

Helena swallowed her will.

 

"Can you talk to Helena?" Kara asked after a few seconds "Is she okay?"

 

"She is..." Helena answered mysteriously. "Leave us alone" She said to a couple of guards who had just entered. They did so without question.

 

"Lena..." Kara sighed taking courage "First of all, I want to apologize... I should have acted earlier against Rhea. Helena tried to warn me, Aunt Astra too... Everyone tried, but I was blinded by a past that doesn't exist..." The blonde hesitated to continue "And I want to ask, beg, that you tell Helena that! Tell her that I understand now what she always wanted"

 

"You understand?” Helena chuckled to herself. It was to be expected that when Kara understood Helena, the brunette would be deconstructing her entire way of thinking... Always out of sync.

 

It was as if Helena and Kara were two explosive forces heading in the same direction on opposite paths. Colliding instead of propelling each other.

 

"Yes" Kara said with passion "She was right"

 

Helena looked up at the roof of the spacecraft, then out the window. They were close to Krypton and she didn't know how she would feel being back home... Here, in this bed, with only Kara Zor-el beside her... It was the first time Helena felt like herself.

 

Not a war general.

Not like a queen.

Just Helena and her childhood friend Kara.

 

She sighed and shook her head.

Kara, however, wasn't seeing Helena.

Lena was who she called... And the queen understood.

Loving Lena Luthor must have been a lot easier than just living with Helena Zor-El.

 

Without the shackles of the past, she could understand why Kara and Lena had been so attracted to each other.

 

"Come here" She said suddenly, wanting to prove a theory. "Here" She patted the corner on the bed when Kara eyed her suspiciously.

 

The blonde sat next to her.

It was the first time in years that Helena had allowed herself to be so close to her wife.

 

With Astra's permission, everything felt lighter.

She didn't feel like she was betraying her mentor by being there for her wife.

 

"Lena? What…" Kara tried, but Helena was faster and pulled her wife against her. Her hand went up to her neck and brought their heads together until their lips were almost touching.

 

Kara pulled away before anything could happen and cleared her throat.

 

Standing beside the bed, her wife blushed.

Helena didn't know what game she was starting with the blonde, she didn't understand her feelings very much, but she knew that now it had started. She didn't want to stop.

 

"Lena, I…" Kara was blushing "I…I'm really glad you're okay… Errr… You know…"

 

"Don't you want to kiss me?" Helena asked, almost torturing her wife. Kara blushed even more. All these years, the brunette has tried to drive the blonde crazy... If only she had known it was that easy...

 

Everything would be different.

 

"It's not that… It's just…" Kara tried again.

 

"Don't you like kissing me?" Helena arched an eyebrow.

 

"Of course I like it! I like it! I mean I like it..." Kara almost choked "When... I..."

 

Okay, she was hyperventilating.

 

"No need to give yourself a heart attack" Helena rolled her eyes "I understand"

 

"You understand?” Kara sighed in relief "It's not that I don't like you, Lena... I do!" She nodded and Helena held her breath "But I'm completely in love with my wife" She confessed.

 

Helena swallowed her surprise.

 

"Everything we did... it wasn't bad and I... thank you for the experience, but..." The blonde cleared her throat "I don't know if Helena will ever forgive me... Rao, I don't know if she even will come back, but she's the one I love"

 

Helena stared at her in silence.

How had an attempt to torture her wife generated such a confession?

 

Rao!!

It was as if the universe was conspiring for Helena to fall into her arms.

 

"What if she never forgives you?" Helena asked curiously. "What if she never wants anything to do with you?"

 

Kara laughed deprecatingly.

 

"It's a big possibility" The blonde sighed "But there's nothing I can do… I’ve loved that woman from the first time I saw her, all those years ago when we were kids. Even in our darkest moments, and in the more acidic words... I've always loved Helena"

 

The queen stared at her in silence.

 

"Your love is a penance"

She concluded.

 

Kara shook her head.

"No, my love is predestined"

 

Helena blinked in surprise.

On Krypton there is a saying, more like a tale, that Rao chooses certain individuals and presents them with soul mates. They are known as predestined loves.

 

Hearing that her wife thinks that of her... Of them.

Helena blinked a few times.

She swallowed.

She let it all go.

Relaxing, she felt a giant weight lift off her shoulders.

 

All these years, the one thing she's always wanted it was for Kara to fight for her. For Krypton. For Kara to let go of her weakness and take what was rightfully hers.

 

The whole time she was trapped by Rhea, Helena prayed that the blonde would break down the doors and burn her enemy.

 

It was like swallowing acid when she saw how she actually behaved.

However, now she was seeing...beginning to see that Kara was indeed fighting for her. Only in a very different way than Helena expected.

 

Every day that Kara continued to love her was another day that the blonde won a battle that Helena had no idea she was fighting.

 

And now, all she wanted was to participate.

 

She is a war general, after all.

 

"She already has”

Helena murmured and looked into her wife's green eyes.

 

"Hm?" Kara frowned, confused. "what?"

 

"She already forgave you" Helena explained "A long time ago... I think she just wasn't ready to admit it"

 

"She said that?" Kara asked suspiciously "Helena said that?"

 

"Hm…" Helena murmured and held out her hand. A gesture Kara should recognize. That's how the blonde asked her to marry her. “Hold my hand"

 

Kara blinked and hesitated.

Trembling, she held Helena's hand gently between hers. A field of electricity raced between them. Something deep, ancient... Fated.

 

Kara widened her eyes.

She took the brunette's hand to her lips and kissed it lightly.

 

"Rao!" She sighed.

 

"The name is actually Helena, dear"

Denying destiny

"Hello, Lena Luthor" Lena opened her eyes "It's nice to finally meet you"

 

Lena watched the being hovering over her bed.

It was Lena's first night back in her apartment and she had trouble sleeping... The bed was different, the smell was different, the world...

 

Helena's presence in her apartment was also striking.

She could see her in every detail...she could feel her superiority in the air she breathed.

 

"Who are you?" Lena narrowed her eyes, returning to the reality that a stranger was in her room. How had he gotten in? Her security system was working... She checked it three times before lying down.

 

"Ah" his eyes sparkled "You may call me Mxy" He pretended to bow.

 

"What do you want?" Lena asked tiredly "If you came to try to kill me, I suggest that you do it quickly and without a speech... I'm not in the mood today" Lena snorted, and it was true. With all the confusion in her mind, she would even welcome an assassination attempt.

 

The man just laughed.

 

"It's amazing how you can be so similar and so different at the same time..." He murmured and Lena started to remember her conversations with Helena. She had commented on that name... He was the one who had made the switch between them.

 

"If you came here to compare me to Helena..." She started to get up from the bed. She felt like ending the spirit of this being that had put her through all of this... Experiencing all of this...

 

"No" He categorically denied "I came to ask you why you are running away from your destiny"

 

Lena blinked in shock.

 

"Sorry, what?"

 

"You two!” He threw his hands up "When it's not one, it's another... When will I have peace?" He muttered more to himself than to Lena.

 

"I'm not running from anything" Lena ignored him.

 

"No?" He smiled "Then why are you running away from Kara Danvers?"

 

Lena froze and glared at him.

"I do not know you!" She began slowly "You are invading my privacy, my home..." her voice cut like ice "And you still have the nerve to question my relationship?"

 

"Yes?" He laughed.

 

Lena saw red.

"Get out of here!" She angrily said "Get out before I call my security"

 

"Oh, Lena, don't be adorable" He sat down on the small sofa near the porch and Lena followed, ready to throw the little being from the window if need be "I just want to help..."

 

"I don't need help" She crossed her arms "Much less yours!"

 

He looked at her for a few seconds.

 

"I wonder…" he began aloud "What is it that's holding you back?"

 

"There's nothing holding me back!" Lena stamped her foot "You have ten seconds to get out of here"

 

"Something is blocking your feelings..." He frowned, ignoring her "Ah..." her eyes twinkled "Could it be..."

 

"Five seconds" Lena repeated, ready to run back to her bed and hit the panic button that would bring all of her security to her room in less than a minute.

 

He raised his arms in a gesture of surrender.

"I’ll see you around, Lena Luthor"

 

He was gone and Lena blinked in bewilderment.

She threw herself on the bed without thinking.

After everything she'd been through... Nothing could shake her anymore, not even the daydreams of a fifth-dimensional being.

 

 

 

 

Lena opened her eyes to a grunt of pain.

Around her, she saw a silhouette fly towards her. Reflexively, she stepped out of the way.

 

"Helena!! Enough! I think you already know that the exchange was beneficial for everyone" The same voice that was in her room. Mxy!

 

"Get up, Lena" Helena pulled her by the arm "Do you want to hit him?"

 

Lena blinked several times, trying to understand what was happening. Her feet felt the grass on the ground and around, she could see a big endless field. Helena was beside her.

 

"No…I…" Speaking was difficult "What's going on?"

 

Helena frowned.

"Your connection is too weak" She supported Lena's weight until the brunette sat down again "What did you do to her?" She turned like an animal towards the goblin.

 

"Nothing! I swear..." He raised his hands "It's only natural that the connection is lost over time... I believe that in a few days you won't be able to communicate anymore"

 

"Never?" Lena's eyes widened...she didn't know how to feel about this. She had gotten used to living in Helena's mind... In Helena's life.

 

She didn't know if she was ready to say goodbye to it all. And she didn't even have the opportunity to say goodbye to Kara...

 

"Breathe" Helena whispered in her direction, her voice so calm it didn't sound like her own. "No..." She hesitated "Is there no way we can see each other again?" The queen asked Mxy.

 

He shook his head sadly.

"Not that I know of" He hesitated "Even though I know the strange nature of your bond"

 

"Strange?" Lena asked with confusion.

 

"There's something... peculiar between you two" her eyes sparkled "I've never seen anything like it, but when I was with you at your house, Lena, I was able to observe it closely"

 

"What?" Helena asked impatiently.

 

"Your connection... It was more than a mental connection" Lena narrowed her eyes, starting to understand what he was getting at "It was physical too"

 

"Obviously" Helena rolled her eyes.

 

"You switched bodies" He laughed maniacally "You felt it in each other's bodies"

 

Helena was silent.

Lena stared at him in disbelief, soon understanding what was happening.

 

"What does that mean?"

 

"It means that you felt each other's feelings, Helena" He explained towards the monarch "Hate, desire, contempt and... love"

 

"I know very well what my feelings are, nothing here belongs to someone else" Helena was the first to protest, expecting Lena's support, but the other brunette just stared at the two.

 

"Is that why I'm so confused? Feeling everything and nothing at the same time?" Lena asked with the urge to cry. It was as if she loved too much and hated too much. She felt it all like a lump of bitterness in her throat. Especially when Kara's name was involved.

 

"Yes" He explained smiling.

 

"That's not possible" Helena denied.

 

"But it is" He continued.

 

"But..." Lena cleared her throat "How am I supposed to know what's me and what's Helena?" She thought of Kara "I don't want to hurt anybody"

 

"You don't have to think about separating those feelings, Lena Luthor" Myx sounded like a fifth dimensional being for the first time since she'd appeared in her room. "You have to balance them"

 

"But if our mental connection is weakening" Helena interrupted "This physical connection will too, won't it?"

 

"Perhaps" He shrugged "But what already happened, shouldn't be changed. Aren't you better now, Helena? After you accepted to listen?"

 

The queen crossed her arms and pretended the answer was no.

 

Lena started to laugh.

Helena looked at her like she was crazy.

Maybe she was.

 

"I'm not better" She whispered "I'm drifting, not knowing... Not understanding"

 

Mxy knelt beside her.

"When you think of Helena, what's the first feeling you think she's feeling?"

 

Lena looked at her.

Imperial.

 

"I don't know... Anger? Grudge?" Not that the brunette didn't have her reasons to feel that way... But that's what Lena thought.

 

"Helena... When you lie down to sleep, do you feel heavy? Distressed?"

 

"No..." The queen said slowly, understanding what the being was getting at.

 

"Lena... Everything Helena feels, you feel too... The difference is that she's not afraid to let it out."

 

"Of hurting other people" Lena corrected.

 

"No" It was Helena who interrupted "Of not letting myself be hurt... If you have anger, Lena, let yourself feel it... Let it swallow you, take your breath away! I let myself be swallowed by resentment for years, But when I took another breath, everything that held me back was gone..."

 

Lena watched her with interest and out of the corner of her eye she saw Mxy smile and disappear.

 

"I overreacted, I confess" The queen fell to her knees in front of her "I went too far with my feelings, but Lena... You are doing exactly the same, just in the opposite direction. I projected onto others and you... Are projecting onto herself. All this anger, this guilt... Feel it and let it go, Lena Luthor"

 

Lena swallowed hard, her eyes watering.

 

"Don't take years to see what's in front of you by being sunk in a sea of pain" Helena smiled sadly "Not like I did"

 

"I don't want to hurt Kara" Lena confessed in a whisper.

 

"She will want to share your pain and not be pushed away" Helena said firmly "Believe me, sending her away in a heroic gesture will hurt more than letting her see that you are not perfect, Lena"

 

"How are you so sure?"

 

"You love her?"

 

Lena has a lot of complex answers to this question, but right now, all she can do is answer as simply as possible.

 

"Yes"

 

Helena smiled, something rare in the monarch's face.

"I'm sure because I know she loves you back"

 

"It's something our Karas share" Lena said looking into her other self's eyes. Different traumas, sharing the same cure. "It's something we both share"

 

Helena didn't deny it.

Midnight Courage

Lena Luthor took three deep breaths before she knocked softly on the wooden door in front of her. Her hands were shaking, from anticipation or from nervousness, she didn't know... The only thing she knew was that she had woken up with a ragged breath and a name on her lips...

 

"kara"

The blonde opened the door.

The surprise on her face was so clear and it seemed to stab straight into the businesswoman's chest.

 

"Lena..." her name was like an answered prayer on the blonde's lips "Is everything okay?"

 

Lena frowned.

Of course Kara would think that it wasn't okay, after all, Lena had been the one who pushed her away... And looking for her in the middle of the night might not be the best way to do that, but she had woken up with that courage and now she couldn’t waste it or wait for common sense to creep into her mind once more.

 

"Sorry I woke you up, I know it's late..." She hesitated "But can we talk?"

 

"Sure" Kara pushed aside and she entered supergirl's small but cozy apartment. "I heard you coming"

 

Of course she had heard.

"Yes...you can hear me from afar" For a moment, Lena forgot about it.

 

Kara Danvers was supergirl!

"Helena..."

 

"Okay" She mumbled irritably "I'll release her, but from today onwards, this type of woman is prohibited from entering the palace"

 

Kara tried to hold back her laughter, but she couldn't.

 

Helena narrowed her eyes at her and Kara stopped laughing immediately at the woman's look.

She had a love for her own life.

 

"You're in charge." She raised her hands in surrender and Helena nodded.

 

"I'm glad we agree” She said and hesitated. She pushed Kara to the side a little and without even breathing too loudly to avoid scaring her, the blonde moved to the side, giving Helena space to lie down next to her.

 

Hard as a rock, full of tension in her shoulders, Helena stretched her legs and lay down on the left side of her wife's bed. They didn't touch, the bed was big enough for that, but just the heat emanating from each other brought comfort to the queens.

 

Helena took a deep breath, counted to ten. When Kara remained silent, she counted to ten again. Her wife's strong and silent presence made her less tense.

 

"I don't know if I'll ever be able to tell you the details of what she did to me..." She began. "I don't want those images in your head, especially now..." She looked towards the blonde's belly. "But what I know is that if I ever want to tell someone, it will be you."

 

Kara swallowed the urge to hug her wife, but she knew that Helena was already making an effort to be lying next to her, she didn't need her touch now, just her understanding.

 

"Whenever you want... If you want..." Kara said firmly "I'll be here"

 

Helena nodded and whispered so low that Kara almost didn't hear it.

"ehlls ghaotived ndivi"

 

A small modification.

'The light stars of my existence.'

Kara

Chapter Notes

See the end of the chapter for notes

Kara Danvers woke up with a sigh, refusing to open her eyes - she'd been doing that a lot lately, staying in bed more and more now that she was sleeping better after sorting things out with Lena. She tried to turn around only to realize that she wasn't lying on her bed. In fact, she was not lying anywhere.

 

Standing, she opened her eyes only to find herself in an unfamiliar room. The room was large, with walls decorated with gold and red threads. And empty except for a bed in the middle of the room, a silhouette seemed to be sleeping there. The first thing Kara saw was the hem of a flowing red dress, which hung over the end of the bed, falling onto the sheets that were also gold. The second thing she noticed was the familiarity of the silhouette and the brunette locks that hung over the bed. Kara approached.

 

"Lena?"

What place was this?

 

"Don't wake her, please" She heard someone talking behind her and turned around quickly, scared. It was difficult for someone to approach her without her knowing. She was immediately alert. "She's exhausted and has finally given in to sleep, I don't want to disturb her rest"

 

Kara blinked several times at the person in front of her.

The person who wore her face.

 

"I don't think we've ever been introduced" She continued, as if she wasn't looking at her doppelganger. Another version of it. "My name is Kara Zor-el, and I am Queen of Krypton"

 

Kara swallowed.

Lena had told her about how this was possible, the whole story with her family... But it was still strange to hear about this reality so different from hers, especially without having experienced it in any way.

But she wouldn't let herself be swayed.

 

"And I'm Kara Zor-el, or Kara Danvers, or Supergirl" She replied and looked at the other version of herself, noticing her posture, her face, her clothes that weren't so strange to her, after all, her childhood had been on Krypton and finally down to her belly...

 

What?

 

"You’re pregnant!"

She said in surprise, finally noticing an indistinguishable belly amidst the blue cloths that covered her. Kara opened and closed her mouth several times so you couldn't understand what she was seeing. Lena hadn't told her any of that. What?

 

"I am" The woman had the audacity to laugh at her shocked expression "A gift from Helena" She shook her head towards the woman sleeping in her bed and Kara had confirmation that it was indeed Helena.

 

"Helena agreed to have a child with you?" Kara asked in disbelief. The last time she had seen the brunette, she didn't even want to stay married, let alone have a child...

 

"We made up" The woman-Kara-How strange this was... she walked to the edge of the bed, watched her wife for a few seconds before placing her hand on her belly, almost unconsciously. "She forgave me and well... We are rebuilding our relationship"

 

"She forgave you out of the blue?"

 

"It wasn't out of the blue" She replied "There were... extenuating circumstances, I was deceived and led to make certain decisions that were not correct"

 

Kara watched her as the woman watched her wife. The look on her face was unmistakable, it was the same way she looked at Lena.

 

"You love her" She deduced.

 

"'More than anything" She pressed her hands over her belly again "Well, almost everything"

 

Kara nodded to herself.

That was good!

The little time she spent with Helena made her see that-despite her bad mood-she deserved to be happy and Supergirl had developed a certain affection for the woman who had the same face as the woman she loved. It was impossible not to care about her and she was happy - genuinely happy - that the other Kara loved her as much as she loved Lena.

 

She only trusted herself to love every version of Lena that existed in the universe.

 

"I couldn't have said it better myself" She said laughing and Kara realized she must have said it out loud. She blushed, but wasn't embarrassed.

 

"What place is this?" She changed the subject. "I didn't know the two of us could connect like they did"

 

"I didn't know either" The queen shrugged and walked away from the bed. "I think we're in my subconscious, well... At least I hope so since Helena is here" She pointed to the sleeping brunette "If it's yours I think we'll have problems"

 

Kara laughed against her will.

“I don’t think we’re on my mind” She took a deep  breath of air. There was something different here... It was as if the air was nostalgic. She found herself remembering when she was four years old and running in the gardens of Krypton.

 

She looked around and saw a sort of balcony, a balcony covered by a large curtain that seemed to have appeared as soon as she turned in that direction. She walked there without thinking twice, aware that another version of her was following her, but not really caring about it. Opening the curtain, she lost her breath.

 

There, just like the necklace she carried as a gift from Lena, was Krypton. In all its glory, with its red sky and thicker air than she was used to. The sun, Rao, the sun and its rays fell on her skin making her murmur and hold tight to the necklace around her neck.

 

She didn't realize she was crying until the woman next to her cleared her throat.

 

"In your universe... Is it really destroyed?"

 

Kara hesitated.

She didn't talk about it, not even with Lena who was the one she trusted most.

But if she couldn't talk to herself...

 

"Yes" She wiped the tears from her cheek "No one will ever understand, not even my cousin..." She hesitated again "He doesn't remember, it wasn't his house, but it was mine... I remember the air, I remember how the ground felt under my feet, I remember how the silk of my clothes fell against my skin, what it was like to think in my mother tongue, what it was like to talk to my family... I remember of everything and every time I remember, it seems like I lose everything again" She swallowed hard the urge to cry "And I feel this... This responsibility to be the only one who remembers, as if it were my duty to keep Krypton alive , because if I don't remember... It seems like it never existed"

 

"I'm very sorry"

That's what she said and Kara nodded, trying to compose herself about Krypton's sun, even if it was in the mind of the other version of her. It was like it was the same, she felt the same.

 

"When Lena came back... She gave me this" She showed the necklace "To remind me, and I..." She took a deep breath "I feel embarrassed to say, that I'm glad she ended up here in this universe - even with everything she and Helena suffered - because now I'm not the only one who remembers and... It's like she shares this responsibility with me and it makes me... Breathe for the first time in years"

 

"I should say this is selfish of you" The queen said laughing lightly to herself "But I can't, because I feel the same" She pointed to the room behind the curtains "This exchange... Gave me back the love of my life" She looked at her belly "It gave me my family" Kara shook her head "I wouldn't trade it for anything so... I don't know if I'll ever be able to talk to your Lena again, but if I don't , tell her that" Kara arched an eyebrow "Tell her that I forgive her for lying, that I understand. That I appreciate it, because without her, Helena would never have come back to me..."

 

"And without you and without Helena, I don't think Lena would have forgiven me" Kara finally admitted to herself "At least not without causing permanent damage to our relationship"

 

The two blondes looked at each other with private understanding.

They knew exactly what each wanted to say to the other.

 

Kara sighed and looked towards the curtains again.

"In another life... I would have loved Helena too"

 

"And in another life, I would have loved Lena"

Zor-el completed.

 

Kara thought that hearing this would make her jealous or upset, but no, she understood exactly what another version of her meant, because there was no Kara, in the any universe, who didn't love a version of Lena.

 

"But that was never our story, was it?"

Kara asked the queen.

 

"No" She denied and looked at the red sky hanging over them. "Because we were always theirs"

Epilogue

As the months went by, things on Krypton calmed down.

 

This world was no longer so dangerous, because Helena no longer let it be. The death of her enemy, even though it initially generated some confusion, had been the main point in uniting the empire under the command of Helena and Kara. Not that Kara was too worried about it, she left everything in Helena's hands because her only concern was growing the heir of Krypton in her womb.

 

And just like Krypton, things inside Helena, more precisely her feelings, also calmed down. As time passed, anger and frustration no longer dominated her actions and thoughts. It had been a long time since Helena felt the same resentment and hurt that had taken hold of her since her father's death. And there was a reason...

 

But that reason was now testing Helena's patience again. For the first time in months, Helena was full of frustration.

 

 

"Aaaaaaaaaa! Rao!!!" She heard her wife scream again and ran her hand over her dress, feeling frustrated at not having anything she could do to help her. Helena had faced armies and empires, but Kara's screams left her paralyzed.

 

"Do something!" She growled in the direction of the doctors monitoring the scene. Two of them looked at each other and one swallowed hard before answering her.

 

“Your Majesty, we are doing our best to make her comfortable”

 

Helena squinted her eyes and grabbed the knife at her waist, just to remind them who they were talking to. The man turned white.

 

"If she dies, so do you…”

 

Everyone in the room looked at each other in fear, but they were saved by her wife. As always... Kara would never let her threaten people in peace anymore.

 

"Helena!!" She managed to grunt from the bed she was lying on in her room. "Stop threatening my doctors! I kind of need them right now"

 

"Then they should do their job and not stand there while you feel pain" Helena said seriously.

 

Kara laughed and raised her hand, calling her closer. Helena walked to the edge of the bed as if walking towards death. She didn't like being there at all.

 

"Helena!"

 

"I said I'd better wait outside..." She sat on the edge of the bed and held her wife's hand. Kara squeezed her so hard that Helena regretted getting close. "You know I hate this kind of thing"

 

"This is your son too! You're going to stay here with me! Stop complaining, it's not even you who's feeling pain" Kara said bravely. Over time, the blonde lost her fear of responding to Helena and gained confidence in challenging the woman.

 

One of the reasons why Helena was frustrated at the moment. Everything was much simpler when everyone was scared to death just hearing her name.

 

She looked at her wife's expression, even though she was talking to Helena like that, Kara's look towards her contained love and euphoria. She looked at her bulging belly and her heart raced thinking about the baby she would soon see.

 

Yes, things were much simpler before.

However, Helena wouldn't trade these complications for anything in the world.

 

"It was you who chose to carry it the old way," She muttered to herself, low enough that Kara didn't hear, but from the way the blonde looked at her, she knew she understood.

 

"You stay" She decided.

 

Helena sighed in defeat as she saw another contraction hit her wife. She frowned in response to Kara's expression and also how tightly the woman squeezed her hand. Rao! If she wasn't used to pain and torture she would have complained.

 

"It's time, Your Majesty" One of the doctors said as he looked at the situation that was happening between her wife's legs. Helena soon turned her gaze back to her face. She didn't even like to imagine what was going on there.

 

She turned pale when she heard the growl Kara was making. Not knowing what to do, she raised her hand and brushed the blonde's hair away from her face.

 

"Your Majesty..."

 

Helena was enchanted by the determination she saw in the woman's eyes. She didn't hear anyone calling her.

 

"Your Majesty..."

 

She caressed her wife's face.

 

"Helena..." Kara squeezed her hand.

 

"Hm, what?" She looked at the doctor "Is everything okay?"

 

He cleared his throat.

"Yes...uh, your majesty, if...um, if your majesty could sit behind your wife...it would help um..."

 

Helena half-closed her eyes and adjusted herself on the bed.

"I hope your skills as a doctor are better than your communication skills" She lifted her wife gently. "Like this?"

 

Helena sat against the headboard with Kara in her arms. It was not an unusual position. As time went by, Kara took advantage of her pregnancy to get hugs from Helena, especially in the early hours of the morning.

 

Helena pretended not to understand her wife's cleverness, and just gave her what she wanted.

 

"A strong one is coming" Kara grumbled and Helena focused her energy on her wife. Almost pushing along with her. Then she heard the blonde scream as she pushed. Their connection was so strong that she almost felt it when the child was being born.

 

If anyone asked her, Helena would say that her son had also been born of her at that moment.

 

The doctor pulled the child by the neck, and Helena could catch a glimpse of the small body in his arms.

 

"Hey! Careful, you idiot!" She grumbled as he gave her son two light slaps on the back. She wanted to kill him right then and there, but luckily for him, she still had Kara in her arms.

 

"Is everything okay? Where’s my son?” Kara asked somewhat incoherently and Helena felt extremely protective of her wife.

 

"Give the child to my wife now" She threatened, but the doctor was too busy finishing care of the newborn.

 

Finally, a loud cry ran through the room and Kara laughed as she heard their child for the first time.

 

"He has your temper" She joked towards her, but Helena was too paralyzed to care. She only knew how to look at the small bundle being placed in Kara's arms.

 

"He's a boy" The doctor said smiling, but Helena didn't pay him any attention. She just looked at her son... They already knew he would be a boy, both by feeling and by the matrix, but it was good to be certain.

 

"Helena..." Kara said crying, with her son in her arms "Thank you... Thank you for this" She only knew how to thank her.

 

Helena seemed to wake up. She gently came out from behind her wife and sat down next to her, kneeling on the bed.

 

"May I...?" She didn't even know what to say, but Kara smiled at her and placed the baby on her arms.

 

The boy stopped crying as soon as Helena held him, opening his big green eyes towards her, curious about the world.

 

Helena lost her breath.

 

Throughout the pregnancy, she said that this child was hers, and it changed her inside. But looking into her son's soul, the truth is that it was Helena that was his. She would do anything for this boy.

 

"I..." She choked in emotions...

 

She looked at Kara who was moved by the scene.

She never thought they would get here.

 

"Thank you" She repeated her wife's words, but couldn't help but add "Thank you for not giving up on me... I... It was all worth it for him"

 

Kara let several  tears flow.

"All of it?”

 

"Everything" Helena confirmed "I forgive you for everything" She felt Kara hold her hand "And Kara...?"

 

The blonde looked towards her in anticipation.

 

Helena looked one last time at her son, a perfect mix of her and her wife and it was impossible not to reveal... It was impossible not to reciprocate the incredible gift that was in her arms. Helena just whispered:

 

"I love you"

 

 

 

 

 

 

Far away, in practically another world, a blonde woman, of medium height, walked quickly through the corridors of L-corp. It was Sunday and she knew no one would be here but Lena Luthor.

 

She only managed to get this far because of everything Lex Luthor had taught her. But this time, she was using her knowledge for a good cause.

 

Lex was dead, but the child she held in her arms was alive. And this child had the right to grow up with all the luxury that his surname could give him.

 

Luthor!

This child was a Luthor.

There was only one person capable of taking care of the baby as he deserved to be taken care of.

 

The baby in her arms started to cry, and the woman had to be quick now. In a flash, she left the child on Lena Luthor's secretary's desk and hid so the CEO wouldn't see her.

 

Hearing a child crying, Lena left her office. She had come on Sunday just to sign a paper and would be heading home. She had agreed with Kara to spend the day watching tv series without working.

 

Looking around, her plans were shattered. There was a child on Jess's desk, her secretary. The baby was crying and Lena responded almost by instinct by picking him up. Inside the cloth that covered him, there was a note.

 

'He's a Luthor! And he should stay with his family'

 

Lena frowned. She didn't know what that meant, but looking at the baby in her lap, who had stopped crying as soon as he saw her, she knew it would work out. If this child was truly a Luthor, she wouldn't leave him alone.

 

With one hand, she took out her cell phone and dialed her number one contact.

 

"Hi baby, tell me you're coming back... I've already prepared the popcorn" She heard her loved one's voice on the phone.

 

"Kara..."

 

"Is everything okay?” The blonde soon realized that something was wrong. "What happened?"

 

Lena swallowed.

 

"I think we got a baby?”
